<ruby><rb>沿邊</rb><rt>YIN-PIN</rt></ruby><ruby><rb>註</rb><rt>CÜ</rt><rb>相對個</rb><rt>SIANG-TE-GO</rt><rb>節章</rb><rt>TSIH-TSÔNG</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>印刷</rb><rt>ING-SHIH</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. I.

<ruby><rb>起初</rb><rt>Kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>創造</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>虛空個</rb><rt>hyü-kʽong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽong-shü</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>運動</rb><rt>yüing-dong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>水個</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>Kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>分出</rb><rt>feng-cʽih</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>日裏</rb><rt>Nyih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>夜裏</rb><rt>Yia-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] m-neh，現代寧波話說 mhiq7，一般作「嘸沒」，本字或爲「嘸得」。</sup>

<sup>\[2\] de，現代寧波話說 lei2，通作「了」，本字或爲「著」。</sup>

<sup>\[3\] keh，指示詞。現代寧波話說 kiq7，一般作「該」，本字爲「個（箇）」。</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>Shü</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>穹蒼</rb><rt>kyʽüong-tsʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分勒開</rb><rt>feng-leh-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>造出</rb><rt>zao-cʽih</rt><rb>穹蒼</rb><rt>kyʽüong-tsʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>穹蒼</rb><rt>kyʽüong-tsʽông</rt><rb>下頭個</rb><rt>ʽô-deo-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>穹蒼</rb><rt>kyʽüong-tsʽông</rt><rb>上頭個</rb><rt>zông-deo-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>穹蒼</rb><rt>kyʽüong-tsʽông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>天下個</rb><rt>Tʽin-ʽô-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>燥個</rb><rt>sao-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>露出來</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>燥個</rb><rt>sao-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>Di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>聚攏東個</rb><rt>jü-long-tong-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>Hæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>地垟</rb><rt>Di-yiang</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>籽個</rb><rt>ts-go</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>各種</rb><rt>koh-cong</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>𣝗</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>地垟</rb><rt>Di-yiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>各種</rb><rt>koh-cong</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>籽個</rb><rt>ts-go</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>各種</rb><rt>koh-cong</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子個</rb><rt>ko-ts-go</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>𣝗</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>天空裏</rb><rt>Tʽin-kʽong-li</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>光</rb><rt>kwông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分出</rb><rt>feng-cʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>時節</rb><rt>z-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>年庚個</rb><rt>nyin-kang-go</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>光</rb><rt>kwông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天空裏</rb><rt>tʽin-kʽong-li</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djoh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>造出</rb><rt>zao-cʽih</rt><rb>亮個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>光</rb><rt>kwông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>光</rb><rt>kwông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>值</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小個</rb><rt>siao-go</rt><rb>光</rb><rt>kwông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>值</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>造出</rb><rt>zao-cʽih</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天空裏</rb><rt>tʽin-kʽong-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djoh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>值</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>值</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分出</rb><rt>feng-cʽih</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第四</rb><rt>di-s</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>水裏</rb><rt>Shü-li</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>上頭個</rb><rt>zông-deo-go</rt><rb>天空裏</rb><rt>tʽin-kʽong-li</rt><rb>飛來飛去</rb><rt>fi-læ-fi-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>創造</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao</rt><rb>各種</rb><rt>koh-cong</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>水裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生出個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>各種</rb><rt>koh-cong</rt><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>茂茂盛盛</rb><rt>meo-meo-zing-zing</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>充滿</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>海裏個</rb><rt>hæ-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地裏</rb><rt>di-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第五</rb><rt>di-ng</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] ng-lah「爾拉」，第二人稱複數，你們。現代寧波話說 naq8，寫作「倷」。</sup>

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>各種</rb><rt>koh-cong</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>箇類</rb><rt>keh-le</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>箇類</rb><rt>keh-le</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt><rb>箇類</rb><rt>keh-le</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>海裏個</rb><rt>hæ-li-go</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>爬個</rb><rt>bô-go</rt><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>創造</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>創造</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>創造</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一男</rb><rt>ih-nen</rt><rb>一女</rb><rt>ih-nyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>茂茂盛盛</rb><rt>meo-meo-zing-zing</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>充滿</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>海裏個</rb><rt>hæ-li-go</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>行動</rb><rt>ʽang-dong</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>遍地</rb><rt>pin-di</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>籽個</rb><rt>ts-go</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>𣝗個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>走獸</rb><rt>tseo-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天空裏</rb><rt>tʽin-kʽong-li</rt><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>造個</rb><rt>zao-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好勒緊</rb><rt>hao-leh-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第六</rb><rt>di-loh</rt><rb>日了</rb><rt>nyih-de</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. II.

<ruby><rb>是介個</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>全全備備</rb><rt>djün-djün-be-be</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>造個</rb><rt>zao-go</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>安息了</rb><rt>en-sih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>祝讚</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尊起個</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇一日</rb><rt>keh-ih-nyih</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>造成</rb><rt>zao-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>安息了</rb><rt>en-sih-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>創造</rb><rt>Tsʽông-zao</rt><rb>天地個</rb><rt>tʽin-di-go</rt><rb>來歷</rb><rt>læ-lih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>地土</rb><rt>Di-tʽu</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>草木</rb><rt>tsʽao-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>落雨</rb><rt>loh-yü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耕種個</rb><rt>kang-cong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>霧露</rb><rt>vu-lu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>滋潤</rb><rt>ts-jing</rt><rb>遍地</rb><rt>pin-di</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>生氣</rb><rt>seng-kyʽi</rt><rb>吹進</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鼻頭管</rb><rt>bih-deo-kwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>靈魂</rb><rt>ling-weng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>獨自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>對手</rb><rt>te-siu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>造出</rb><rt>zao-cʽih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>走獸</rb><rt>tseo-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領到</rb><rt>ling-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>謳個</rb><rt>eo-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>對手</rb><rt>te-siu</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>煞覺</rb><rt>sah-kao</rt><rb>睏熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>睏熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>肋排骨</rb><rt>leh-ba-kweh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>補好</rb><rt>pu-hao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>馱出個</rb><rt>do-cʽih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇根</rb><rt>keh-keng</rt><rb>肋排骨</rb><rt>leh-ba-kweh</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>骨頭個</rb><rt>kweh-deo-go</rt><rb>骨頭</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>肉個</rb><rt>nyüoh-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>男人個</rb><rt>nen-nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>馱出個</rb><rt>do-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爹孃</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>一體</rb><rt>ih-tʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>夫妻</rb><rt>fu-tsʽi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>出身露體</rb><rt>cʽih-sing-lu-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m̆-kao</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-wô* Üô-tông. 或話<u>亞當</u>。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh-wô* Keh liang-go nying, ziu-z Üô-tông teng gyi tsʽi-ts dô-kô. 或話，箇兩個人，就是<u>亞當</u>等其妻子大家。（原註）</sup>

### Ts. III.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>活物</rb><rt>weh-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>調皮</rb><rt>diao-bi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>園</rb><rt>Yün</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>園</rb><rt>Yün</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>園</rb><rt>yün</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>箇株</rb><rt>keh-cü</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>摸</rb><rt>moh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>蛇</rb><rt>Dzô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>未必</rb><rt>vi-pih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>喫仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>善惡</rb><rt>jün-ôh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>箇株</rb><rt>keh-cü</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好喫</rb><rt>hao-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>企慕</rb><rt>kyʽi-mo</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>摘點</rb><rt>tsah-tin</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>開了</rb><rt>kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>纔始</rb><rt>dzæ-s</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>出身露體</rb><rt>cʽih-sing-lu-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>葉</rb><rt>yih</rt><rb>絞帶攏</rb><rt>gao-tæn-long</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>裙</rb><rt>gyüing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>冷陰</rb><rt>lang-ing</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>園</rb><rt>yün</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>幽來</rb><rt>iu-læ</rt><rb>園個</rb><rt>yün-go</rt><rb>樹縫裏</rb><rt>jü-vong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>避過</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>？」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>園裏</rb><rt>yün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>出身露體</rb><rt>cʽih-sing-lu-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>幽過了</rb><rt>iu-ko-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出身露體</rb><rt>cʽih-sing-lu-tʽi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>禁</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>喫過了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko-de</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>配撥</rb><rt>pʽe-peh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>給點</rb><rt>coh-tin</rt><rb>箇株</rb><rt>keh-cü</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>蛇</rb><rt>Dzô</rt><rb>誘惑</rb><rt>yiu-ʽoh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>凡百個</rb><rt>væn-pah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>走路</rb><rt>tseo-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一生一世</rb><rt>ih-sang-ih-si</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>怨仇</rb><rt>ün-dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人個</rb><rt>nyü-nying-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>怨仇</rb><rt>ün-dziu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>傷</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>傷</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>腳後跟</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽeo-keng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕個</rb><rt>sing-yüing-go</rt><rb>難頭</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>喫苦</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kwʽu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>管理</rb><rt>kwun-li</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>妻子個</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>禁</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>土產</rb><rt>tʽu-tsʽæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>一生一世</rb><rt>ih-sang-ih-si</rt><rb>辛苦</rb><rt>sing-kwʽu</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>Tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>野草</rb><rt>yia-tsʽao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>發出來</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>面孔</rb><rt>Min-kʽong</rt><rb>汗出湯流</rb><rt>ʽen-cʽih-tʽô-liu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歸土</rb><rt>kwe-tʽu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>出來個</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏娃</rb><rt>Yüô-w̆ô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>活人個</rb><rt>weh-nying-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皮個</rb><rt>bi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] tʽing-meng「聽聞」，現代寧波話說 thin min，一般作「聽明」。</sup>

<sup>\[2\] ʽah-li，現代寧波話說 aq li，一般作「阿裏」，本字爲「何裏」。</sup>

<sup>\[3\] *Ziu-z* Weh-ming ka-shih. 就是活命解說。（原註）</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>善惡</rb><rt>jün-ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>摘</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃田</rb><rt>Yiæ-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>園</rb><rt>yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耕種</rb><rt>kang-cong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>出來個</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃田</rb><rt>Yiæ-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>園</rb><rt>yün</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>立了</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基路冰</rb><rt>Kyi-lu-ping</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>火焰個</rb><rt>ho-yin-go</rt><rb>寶劍</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt><rb>抈來</rb><rt>yüih-læ</rt><rb>抈去</rb><rt>yüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>攔住</rb><rt>lah-djü</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. IV.

<u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏娃</rb><rt>Yüô-w̆ô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>夏娃</rb><rt>Yüô-w̆ô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>保佑</rb><rt>pao-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>耕</rb><rt>kang</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>土產</rb><rt>tʽu-tsʽæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭生個</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>羊油</rb><rt>yiang-yiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>中意</rb><rt>cong-i</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>中意</rb><rt>cong-i</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>生氣</rb><rt>sang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>變色</rb><rt>pin-seh</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>生氣</rb><rt>sang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>變色</rb><rt>pin-seh</rt></ruby>？<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>行善</rb><rt>ʽang-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>舉</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>作惡</rb><rt>tsoh-ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏來</rb><rt>voh-læ</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講講</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>\[1\] *Kæ-ing-go i-s* Ziu-z teh-djoh. <u>該隱</u>個意思就是得着。（原註）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗曉得</rb><rt>Feh-hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>阿弟個</rb><rt>ah-di-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>開口</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽeo</rt><rb>吞落</rb><rt>tʽeng-loh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿弟個</rb><rt>ah-di-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>親手</rb><rt>tsʽing-siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>流出個</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>耕田</rb><rt>kang-din</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出力</rb><rt>cʽih-lih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>𧻴趤</rb><rt>lông-dông</rt><rb>子</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當弗起</rb><rt>tông-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>𧻴趤</rb><rt>lông-dông</rt><rb>子</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>碰着個</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>Long-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>七倍</rb><rt>tsʽih-be</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃田</rb><rt>Yiæ-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪得</rb><rt>Nô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>有身</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>Yi-noh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以諾</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-noh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>Yi-noh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以拉</rb><rt>Yi-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>以拉</rb><rt>Yi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米戶雅利</rb><rt>Mi-wu-yüô-li</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>米戶雅利</rb><rt>Mi-wu-yüô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪土撒利</rb><rt>Mô-tʽu-sah-li</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>瑪土撒利</rb><rt>Mô-tʽu-sah-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞大</rb><rt>Üô-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>洗拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞大</rb><rt>Üô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅八</rb><rt>Yüô-pah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>帳篷裏</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶八</rb><rt>Yiu-pah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>彈琴</rb><rt>dæn-gying</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>笛子</rb><rt>dih-ts</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>洗拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>土八該隱</rb><rt>Tʽu-pah-kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>銅匠</rb><rt>dong-ziang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>鐵匠</rb><rt>tʽih-ziang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>始祖</rb><rt>s-tsu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>土八該隱</rb><rt>Tʽu-pah-kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿瑪</rb><rt>Nô-mô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞大</rb><rt>Üô-da</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>洗拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>打傷</rb><rt>tang-sông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>七倍</rb><rt>tsʽih-be</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>七十七</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>設</rb><rt>Shih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該隱</rb><rt>Kæ-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>殺掉個</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>設</rb><rt>Shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以挪士</rb><rt>Yi-no-z</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>哀求</rb><rt>æ-gyiu</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. V.

<ruby><rb><u>亞當</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-tông-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>家譜</rb><rt>kô-pu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>創造</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>創造</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>創造一男</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao-ih-nen</rt><rb>一女</rb><rt>ih-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>創造</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ôh-tsia* Nying. 或者人。（原註）</sup>

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>設</rb><rt>Shih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>設</rb><rt>Shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>八百</rb><rt>pah-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>九百</rb><rt>kyiu-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>設</rb><rt>Shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>五歲</rb><rt>ng-shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以挪士</rb><rt>Yi-no-z</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>設</rb><rt>Shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以挪士</rb><rt>Yi-no-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>八百</rb><rt>pah-pah</rt><rb>零七</rb><rt>ling-tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb><u>設</u>個</rb><rt>Shih-go</rt><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>九百</rb><rt>kyiu-pah</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以挪士</rb><rt>Yi-no-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>九十</rb><rt>kyiu-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該南</rb><rt>Kæ-nen</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以挪士</rb><rt>Yi-no-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該南</rb><rt>Kæ-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>八百</rb><rt>pah-pah</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以挪士</rb><rt>Yi-no-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>九百</rb><rt>kyiu-pah</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>該南</rb><rt>Kæ-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪勒列</rb><rt>Mô-leh-lih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>該南</rb><rt>Kæ-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪勒列</rb><rt>Mô-leh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>八百</rb><rt>pah-pah</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>該南</rb><rt>Kæ-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>九百</rb><rt>kyiu-pah</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>十</rb><rt>jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>瑪勒列</rb><rt>Mô-leh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>六十五</rb><rt>loh-jih-ng</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>Yüô-lih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>瑪勒列</rb><rt>Mô-leh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>Yüô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>八百</rb><rt>pah-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>瑪勒列</rb><rt>Mô-leh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>八百</rb><rt>pah-pah</rt><rb>九十五</rb><rt>kyiu-jih-ng</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>Yüô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>六十二</rb><rt>loh-jih-nyi</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>Yi-noh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>Yüô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>Yi-noh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>八百</rb><rt>pah-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>Yüô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>九百</rb><rt>kyiu-pah</rt><rb>六十二</rb><rt>loh-jih-nyi</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>Yi-noh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>六十五</rb><rt>loh-jih-ng</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪土撒拉</rb><rt>Mô-tʽu-sah-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>Yi-noh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪土撒拉</rb><rt>Mô-tʽu-sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來往</rb><rt>læ-w̆ông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三百</rb><rt>sæn-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>Yi-noh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>三百</rb><rt>sæn-pah</rt><rb>六十五</rb><rt>loh-jih-ng</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以諾</rb><rt>Yi-noh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來往</rb><rt>læ-w̆ông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>瑪土撒拉</rb><rt>Mô-tʽu-sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>八十七</rb><rt>pah-jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>瑪土撒拉</rb><rt>Mô-tʽu-sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七百</rb><rt>tsʽih-pah</rt><rb>八十二</rb><rt>pah-jih-nyi</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>瑪土撒拉</rb><rt>Mô-tʽu-sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>九百</rb><rt>kyiu-pah</rt><rb>六十九</rb><rt>loh-jih-kyiu</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>八十二</rb><rt>pah-jih-nyi</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>手裏個</rb><rt>siu-li-go</rt><rb>勞苦</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>咒罵個</rb><rt>tsiu-mô-go</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五百</rb><rt>ng-pah</rt><rb>九十五</rb><rt>kyiu-jih-ng</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉麥</rb><rt>Lah-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>七百</rb><rt>tsʽih-pah</rt><rb>七十七</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Nô-üô i-s* Ziu-z en-w̆e. <u>挪亞</u>意思就是安慰。（原註）</sup>

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>五百</rb><rt>ng-pah</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>閃</rb><rt>Sin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>含</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅弗</rb><rt>Yüô-feh</rt></ruby></u>。

### Ts. VI.

<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>囡個</rb><rt>nön-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>企慕個</rb><rt>kyʽi-mo-go</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>寬限其</rb><rt>kwʽun-ʽæn-gyi</rt><rb>一百廿</rb><rt>ih-pah-nyiæn</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>長大</rb><rt>dziang-do</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>古時節</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名聲個</rb><rt>ming-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>惡貫滿盈</rb><rt>ôh-kwun-mun-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>存的個</rb><rt>dzeng-tih-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>作惡</rb><rt>tsoh-ôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>懊悔</rb><rt>ao-hwe</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>造出</rb><rt>zao-cʽih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>創造個</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>走獸</rb><rt>tseo-siu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>創造</rb><rt>tsʽông-zao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>懊悔了</rb><rt>ao-hwe-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>行實</rb><rt>ʽang-jih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>全備</rb><rt>djün-be</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來往</rb><rt>læ-w̆ông</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>閃</rb><rt>Sin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>含</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅弗</rb><rt>Yüô-feh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>變壞了</rb><rt>pin-wæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>強橫霸道</rb><rt>gyiang-wang-pô-dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>變壞</rb><rt>pin-wæ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>變掉</rb><rt>pin-diao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>meh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>強橫霸道</rb><rt>gyiang-wang-pô-dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>松樹</rb><rt>song-jü</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>房間</rb><rt>vông-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>松香</rb><rt>song-hyiang</rt><rb>裏外</rb><rt>li-nga</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>泥好</rb><rt>nyi-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇隻</rb><rt>Keh-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>打法</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>三百</rb><rt>sæn-pah</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby><sup> <ruby><rb>船裏</rb><rt>Jün-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>窗</rb><rt>cʽông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分出</rb><rt>feng-cʽih</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>中</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三層</rb><rt>sæn-zeng</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽong-shü</rt><rb>氾漲</rb><rt>fæn-tsiang</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>生氣個</rb><rt>seng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血氣</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>每樣</rb><rt>me-yiang</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一雌</rb><rt>ih-tsʽ</rt><rb>一雄</rb><rt>ih-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>積落</rb><rt>tsih-loh</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>糧草</rb><rt>liang-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>依從</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. VII.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾自</rb><rt>Ng-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落雨</rb><rt>loh-yü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>除滅掉</rb><rt>djü-mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介做</rb><rt>ka-tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽong-shü</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>潔淨個</rb><rt>Kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>弗潔淨個</rb><rt>feh-kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>爬個</rb><rt>bô-go</rt><rb>蟲</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>雌雄</rb><rt>tsʽ-yüong</rt><rb>對打對</rb><rt>te-tang-te</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>挪亞</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-üô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽong-shü</rt><rb>沒上</rb><rt>meh-zông</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb><u>挪亞</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-üô-go</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>六百</rb><rt>loh-pah</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>二月</rb><rt>nyi-yüih</rt><rb>十七</rb><rt>jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海洋個</rb><rt>hæ-yiang-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt><rb>崩開</rb><rt>pong-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天窗</rb><rt>tʽin-cʽông</rt><rb>破漏</rb><rt>pʽo-leo</rt></ruby>。
