<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>箇類</rb><rt>keh-le</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>箇類</rb><rt>keh-le</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>爬個</rb><rt>bô-go</rt><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt><rb>箇類</rb><rt>keh-le</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt><rb>箇類</rb><rt>keh-le</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>生氣個</rb><rt>seng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>對打對</rb><rt>te-tang-te</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>勒</rb><rt>leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>走進個</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雌</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽOng-shü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>氾上來</rb><rt>fæn-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>浮起</rb><rt>vu-kyʽi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>Di-zông-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>溢上來</rb><rt>kah-zông-læ</rt><rb>極其</rb><rt>gyih-gyi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>氽來</rb><rt>tʽeng-læ</rt><rb>氽去</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>Di-zông-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>氾上來</rb><rt>fæn-zông-læ</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>天下個</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô-go</rt><rb>高山</rb><rt>kao-sæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>浸沒</rb><rt>tsing-meh</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽOng-shü</rt><rb>蓋上</rb><rt>kæ-zông</rt><rb>高如</rb><rt>kao-jü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>頂高個</rb><rt>ting-kao-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>阿買</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>Shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>動個</rb><rt>dong-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>浸煞</rb><rt>tsing-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>爬個</rb><rt>bô-go</rt><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>個打個個</rb><rt>ko-tang-ko-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>燥地裏</rb><rt>sao-di-li</rt><rb>鼻頭管</rb><rt>bih-deo-kwun</rt><rb>回</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>通</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>生氣</rb><rt>seng-kyʽi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽOng-shü</rt><rb>吞掉</rb><rt>tʽeng-diao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>除滅掉</rb><rt>djü-mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>船裏個</rb><rt>jün-li-go</rt><rb>剩落東</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>Di-zông-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>氾漲</rb><rt>fæn-tsiang</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ts. VIII.

<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>衆生</rb><rt>cong-sang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>發風</rb><rt>fah-fong</rt><rb>吹來</rb><rt>cʽü-læ</rt><rb>吹去</rb><rt>cʽü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>退了</rb><rt>tʽe-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>海裏個</rb><rt>hæ-li-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt><rb>塞住</rb><rt>seh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天窗</rb><rt>tʽin-cʽông</rt><rb>關好</rb><rt>kwæn-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>落雨了</rb><rt>loh-yü-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>Di-zông-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>漸漸</rb><rt>dzin-dzin</rt><rb>退落去</rb><rt>tʽe-loh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>缺勒點了</rb><rt>kyʽüih-leh-tin-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>七月</rb><rt>tsʽih-yüih</rt><rb>十七</rb><rt>jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拉臘</rb><rt>Üô-lah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>Shü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>漸漸</rb><rt>dzin-dzin</rt><rb>退落</rb><rt>tʽe-loh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>十月</rb><rt>jih-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十月</rb><rt>jih-yüih</rt><rb>初一個</rb><rt>tsʽu-ih-go</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-ting</rt><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>推開</rb><rt>tʽe-kʽæ</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>做的個</rb><rt>tso-tih-go</rt><rb>窗門</rb><rt>cʽông-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>放出</rb><rt>Fông-cʽih</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>老鴉</rb><rt>lao-ô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>飛來</rb><rt>fi-læ</rt><rb>飛去</rb><rt>fi-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>燥</rb><rt>sao</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>放出</rb><rt>fông-cʽih</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>試試看</rb><rt>s-s-kʽen</rt><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>退了</rb><rt>tʽe-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djoh</rt><rb>停腳個</rb><rt>ding-kyiah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>遍地</rb><rt>pin-di</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>剩落個</rb><rt>dzing-loh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘴鉗<sup> </sup>裏</rb><rt>cü-gyin-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>新葉</rb><rt>sing-yih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>含</u>的</rb><rt>ʽen-tih</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>退落</rb><rt>tʽe-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>七日</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>放出</rb><rt>fông-cʽih</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 喙</sup>

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>六百</rb><rt>loh-pah</rt><rb>零一</rb><rt>ling-ih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>正月</rb><rt>tsing-yüih</rt><rb>初一</rb><rt>tsʽu-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>燥了</rb><rt>sao-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𢳚開</rb><rt>hyiao-kʽæ</rt><rb>船個</rb><rt>jün-go</rt><rb>蓋</rb><rt>kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>燥了</rb><rt>sao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>二月</rb><rt>nyi-yüih</rt><rb>廿七</rb><rt>nyiæn-tsʽih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>粉燥</rb><rt>feng-sao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>爬個</rb><rt>bô-go</rt><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生養</rb><rt>sang-yiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>茂茂盛盛</rb><rt>meo-meo-zing-zing</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>各種</rb><rt>koh-cong</rt><rb>走獸</rb><rt>tseo-siu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>爬個</rb><rt>bô-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>樣打樣</rb><rt>yiang-tang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>潔淨個</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>潔淨個</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>壇上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>嗅着</rb><rt>hyüong-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>香氣</rb><rt>hyiang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：（<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惡個</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>再弗</rb><rt>tsæ-feh</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>所做</rb><rt>sô-tso</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耕種</rb><rt>kang-cong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>冷</rb><rt>lang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>熱</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>冬</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>夏</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>歇</rb><rt>hyih</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. IX.

<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福</rb><rt>foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>茂茂盛盛</rb><rt>meo-meo-zing-zing</rt><rb>多起</rb><rt>to-kyʽi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>庉滿</rb><rt>deng-mun</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>地裏個</rb><rt>di-li-go</rt><rb>走獸</rb><rt>tseo-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>海裏個</rb><rt>hæ-li-go</rt><rb>魚</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>性命個</rb><rt>sing-ming-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>走獸</rb><rt>tseo-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟個</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>流出</rb><rt>Liu-cʽih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>茂茂盛盛</rb><rt>meo-meo-zing-zing</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生滿</rb><rt>sang-mun</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>將來個</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>飛禽</rb><rt>fi-gying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>走出來個</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>地垟裏個</rb><rt>di-yiang-li-go</rt><rb>走獸</rb><rt>tseo-siu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>再弗</rb><rt>tsæ-feh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽong-shü</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>肉身個</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再弗</rb><rt>tsæ-feh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽong-shü</rt><rb>壞掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>鱟</rb><rt>heo</rt><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>雲裏</rb><rt>yüing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>立約個</rb><rt>lih-iah-go</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>Tsiang-læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>地垟個</rb><rt>di-yiang-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>鱟</rb><rt>heo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>立個</rb><rt>lih-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽong-shü</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>鱟</rb><rt>Heo</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>雲裏</rb><rt>yüing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看仔</rb><rt>kʽen-ts</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>設立個</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>立個</rb><rt>lih-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb><u>挪亞</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-üô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出個</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>閃</rb><rt>Sin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>含</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅弗</rb><rt>Yüô-feh</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>含</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦南</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>遍天下</rb><rt>pin-tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>種</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>園</rb><rt>yün</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>喫醉</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>帳篷裏</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-li</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>出間</rb><rt>cʽih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb><u>迦南</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>含</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>出身露體</rb><rt>cʽih-sing-lu-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>閃</rb><rt>Sin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅弗</rb><rt>Yüô-feh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>肩胛頭</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒退步</rb><rt>tao-tʽe-bu</rt><rb>走起</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蓋</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>出身露體</rb><rt>cʽih-sing-lu-tʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>醒轉來</rb><rt>sing-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>方纔</rb><rt>fông-dzæ</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>祝謝</rb><rt>Coh-zia</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>閃</u>個</rb><rt>Sin-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>！<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅弗</rb><rt>Yüô-feh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>閃</u>個</rb><rt>Sin-go</rt><rb>帳篷裏</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽOng-shü</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>三百</rb><rt>sæn-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb><u>挪亞</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-üô-go</rt><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>九百</rb><rt>kyiu-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ts. X.

<ruby><rb><u>挪亞</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-üô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>閃</rb><rt>Sin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>含</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>雅弗</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-feh-go</rt><rb>家譜</rb><rt>kô-pu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽong-shü</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb><u>雅弗</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-feh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>歌篾</rb><rt>Ko-mih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>瑪葛</rb><rt>Mô-keh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>瑪代</rb><rt>Mô-dæ</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅番</rb><rt>Yüô-fæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>土八</rb><rt>Tʽu-pah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>米設</rb><rt>Mi-shih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>提拉</rb><rt>Di-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb><u>歌篾</u>個</rb><rt>Ko-mih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞實基拿</rb><rt>Üô-jih-kyi-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利法</rb><rt>Li-fah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>陀迦瑪</rb><rt>Do-kyüô-mô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb><u>雅番</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-fæn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以利沙</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>大失</rb><rt>Da-shih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基帝</rb><rt>Kyi-ti</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>多單</rb><rt>To-tæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>近</rb><rt>gying</rt><rb>海個</rb><rt>hæ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各打各個</rb><rt>koh-tang-koh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>族種</rb><rt>dzoh-cong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>房分</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb><u>含</u>個</rb><rt>ʽEn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>古實</rb><rt>Kwu-jih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>麥西</rb><rt>Mah-si</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb><u>古實</u>個</rb><rt>Kwu-jih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西巴</rb><rt>Si-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈腓拉</rb><rt>Ha-fi-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>撒大</rb><rt>Sah-da</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拉瑪</rb><rt>Lah-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>撒提迦</rb><rt>Sah-di-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>拉瑪</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-mô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>示巴</rb><rt>Z-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>底但</rb><rt>Ti-dæn</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>古實</rb><rt>Kwu-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>寧錄</rb><rt>Nying-loh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>勇勇</rb><rt>üong-üong</rt><rb>打獵個</rb><rt>tang-liah-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>俗話</rb><rt>Dzoh-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>Ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>寧錄</rb><rt>Nying-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>勇勇</rb><rt>üong-üong</rt><rb>打獵個</rb><rt>tang-liah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>開國</rb><rt>kʽæ-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士拿</rb><rt>Z-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴別</rb><rt>Pô-bih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以力</rb><rt>Yi-lih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞甲</rb><rt>Üô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>甲尼</rb><rt>Kyiah-nyi</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞述</rb><rt>Üô-jih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼尼微</rb><rt>Nyi-nyi-vi</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>利河伯</rb><rt>Li-ʽo-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦拉</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼尼微</rb><rt>Nyi-nyi-vi</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>迦拉</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央個</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利鮮</rb><rt>Li-sin</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>麥西</rb><rt>Mah-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路丁</rb><rt>Lu-ting</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>安南</rb><rt>En-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利哈</rb><rt>Li-ha</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>納土</rb><rt>Neh-tʽu</rt></ruby></u>、<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>伯魯</rb><rt>Pah-lu</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>甲路</rb><rt>Kyiah-lu</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>葛托</rb><rt>Keh-tʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>甲路</rb><rt>Kyiah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一族</rb><rt>ih-dzoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西頓</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>革迦撒</rb><rt>Keh-kyüô-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>、<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞基</rb><rt>Üô-kyi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西尼</rb><rt>Si-nyi</rt></ruby></u>、<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞華底</rb><rt>Üô-wô-ti</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西瑪利</rb><rt>Si-mô-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈默</rb><rt>Ha-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>延開去</rb><rt>yin-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb><u>迦南</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西頓</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其臘</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>其臘</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦薩</rb><rt>Kyüô-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>蛾摩拉</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拉瑪</rb><rt>Lah-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>洗扁</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉沙</rb><rt>Lah-sô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>含</u>個</rb><rt>ʽEn-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>族種</rb><rt>dzoh-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文此處錯印爲 Keh-kyüô-seh，後文爲 Keh-kyüô-sah.</sup>

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb><u>雅弗</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-feh-go</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>閃</rb><rt>Sin</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯</rb><rt>Hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族個</rb><rt>keh-dzoh-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb><u>閃</u>個</rb><rt>Sin-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以攔</rb><rt>Yi-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞述</rb><rt>Üô-jih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞法撒</rb><rt>Üô-fah-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>路德</rb><rt>Lu-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞蘭</rb><rt>Üô-læn</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb><u>亞蘭</u>個</rb><rt>üô-læn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>烏士</rb><rt>U-z</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>戶勒</rb><rt>Wu-leh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>其帖</rb><rt>Gyi-tʽiah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>瑪實</rb><rt>Mô-jih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞法撒</rb><rt>Üô-fah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯</rb><rt>Hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希伯</rb><rt>Hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法勒</rb><rt>Fah-leh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>分散了</rb><rt>feng-sæn-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約丹</rb><rt>Iah-tæn</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約丹</rb><rt>Iah-tæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩塔</rb><rt>Üô-mo-tʽah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>沙列</rb><rt>Sô-lih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈薩瑪非</rb><rt>Ha-sah-mô-fi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶拉</rb><rt>Yiæ-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哈多蘭</rb><rt>Ha-to-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>烏薩</rb><rt>U-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>特拉</rb><rt>Deh-lah</rt></ruby>、<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>倭柏</rb><rt>O-pah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞比瑪利</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mô-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>示巴</rb><rt>Z-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>倭非</rb><rt>O-fi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈腓拉</rb><rt>Ha-fi-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>約伯</rb><rt>Iah-pah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約丹</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-tæn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米沙</rb><rt>Mi-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西發</rb><rt>Si-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>閃</u>個</rb><rt>Sin-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>族種</rb><rt>dzoh-cong</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Fah-leh i-s* ziu-z Feng-kʽæ. <u>法勒</u>意思就是分開。（原註）</sup>

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>上文</rb><rt>Zông-veng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亞</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>家譜</rb><rt>kô-pu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽOng-shü</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ts. XI.

<ruby><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽEn-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>搬過去</rb><rt>pun-ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士拿</rb><rt>Z-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>磚頭</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>燒</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>磚頭</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>石油</rb><rt>zah-yiu</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>石灰</rb><rt>zah-hwe</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>塔</rb><rt>tʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djoh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>揚</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-loh-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>造個</rb><rt>zao-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等塔</rb><rt>teng-tʽah</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m̆-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>降落去</rb><rt>kông-loh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>混雜</rb><rt>weng-dzeh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相通</rb><rt>siang-tʽong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>城子個</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歇了</rb><rt>hyih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴別</rb><rt>Pô-bih</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>混雜</rb><rt>weng-dzeh</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Pô-bih i-s* ziu-z Weng-dzeh. <u>巴別</u>意思就是混雜。（原註）</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb><u>閃</u>個</rb><rt>Sin-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>洪水</rb><rt>ʽOng-shü</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>兩年</rb><rt>liang-nyin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>閃</rb><rt>Sin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞法撒</rb><rt>Üô-fah-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>閃</rb><rt>Sin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞法撒</rb><rt>Üô-fah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五百</rb><rt>ng-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞法撒</rb><rt>Üô-fah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三十五</rb><rt>sæn-jih-ng</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞法撒</rb><rt>Üô-fah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯</rb><rt>Hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯</rb><rt>Hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希伯</rb><rt>Hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三十四</rb><rt>sæn-jih-s</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法勒</rb><rt>Fah-leh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希伯</rb><rt>Hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法勒</rb><rt>Fah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法勒</rb><rt>Fah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉苟</rb><rt>Lah-keo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法勒</rb><rt>Fah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉苟</rb><rt>Lah-keo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉苟</rb><rt>Lah-keo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三十二</rb><rt>sæn-jih-nyi</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒鹿</rb><rt>Sah-loh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉苟</rb><rt>Lah-keo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒鹿</rb><rt>Sah-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒鹿</rb><rt>Sah-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿鶴</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒鹿</rb><rt>Sah-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿鶴</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拿鶴</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>廿九</rb><rt>nyiæn-kyiu</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>他拉</rb><rt>Tʽa-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拿鶴</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>他拉</rb><rt>Tʽa-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>十九</rb><rt>jih-kyiu</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>他拉</rb><rt>Tʽa-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拿鶴</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈蘭</rb><rt>Ha-læn</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>他拉</rb><rt>Tʽa-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>他拉</rb><rt>Tʽa-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拿鶴</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈蘭</rb><rt>Ha-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>哈蘭</rb><rt>Ha-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哈蘭</rb><rt>Ha-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒底</rb><rt>Kyüô-leh-ti</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>珥</u>個</rb><rt>R-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿鶴</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>亞伯蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>拿鶴</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以士迦</rb><rt>Yi-z-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哈蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Ha-læn-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>他拉</rb><rt>Tʽa-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>孫子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哈蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Ha-læn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>），<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>），<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒底</rb><rt>Kyüô-leh-ti</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>珥</u>個</rb><rt>R-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈蘭</rb><rt>Ha-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>他拉</rb><rt>Tʽa-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>他拉</rb><rt>Tʽa-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈蘭</rb><rt>Ha-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文此處錯印爲 Kyüô-lah-ti，後文爲 Kyüô-leh-ti.</sup>

### Ts. XII.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>本國</rb><rt>peng-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>揚</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>福氣個</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>根頭</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>出門去</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哈蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Ha-læn-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七十五</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-ng</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>姪子</rb><rt>dzih-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈蘭</rb><rt>Ha-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>積落個</rb><rt>tsih-loh-go</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>敘劍</rb><rt>Jü-kyin</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩利</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-li-go</rt><rb>橡樹</rb><rt>ziang-jü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>現出來個</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>搬到</rb><rt>pun-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>東邊個</rb><rt>tong-pin-go</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>西邊</rb><rt>si-pin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亥</rb><rt>ʽÆ</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>搬過去</rb><rt>pun-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>Kwʽa</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>好看個</rb><rt>hao-kʽen-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> <sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>大臣</rb><rt>da-dzing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>王宮裏</rb><rt>wông-kong-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>雌雄</rb><rt>tsʽ-yüong</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>話向</rb><rt>wô-hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>？<sup>19</sup> ### Ts. XIII.

<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所有個</rb><rt>sô-yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>南地</rb><rt>Nen-di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>富足</rb><rt>fu-tsoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>南地</rb><rt>Nen-di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搬到</rb><rt>pun-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亥</u>個</rb><rt>ʽÆ-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>作惡</rb><rt>tsoh-ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起來</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>東</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>南</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>西</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>北</rb><rt>poh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>地垟裏個</rb><rt>di-yiang-li-go</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>數勒遍</rb><rt>su-leh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>數勒出</rb><rt>su-leh-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>闊</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>搬</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯崙</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>幔利</u>個</rb><rt>Mæn-li-go</rt><rb>橡樹</rb><rt>ziang-jü</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ts. XIV.

<sup>1</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>暗拉非</rb><rt>En-lah-fi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士拿</rb><rt>Z-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞略</rb><rt>Üô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以拉撒</rb><rt>Yi-lah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基大老瑪</rb><rt>Kyi-da-lao-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以攔</rb><rt>Yi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>提達</rb><rt>Di-dah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>列邦</rb><rt>Lih-pông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>征伐</rb><rt>tsing-vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比拉</rb><rt>Pi-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>蛾摩拉</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比沙</rb><rt>Pi-sô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>押瑪</rb><rt>Iah-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士納</rb><rt>Z-neh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>洗扁</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士米別</rb><rt>Z-mi-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比拉</rb><rt>Pi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。（<u><ruby><rb>比拉</rb><rt>Pi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑣珥</rb><rt>So-r</rt></ruby></u>。）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西定</rb><rt>Si-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山嶴</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>鹽</rb><rt>Yin</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基大老瑪</rb><rt>Kyi-da-lao-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>十三</rb><rt>jih-sæn</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>背叛</rb><rt>be-bun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基大老瑪</rb><rt>Kyi-da-lao-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>結黨個</rb><rt>kyih-tông-go</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打敗</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利乏音</rb><rt>Li-vah-ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞提律迦</rb><rt>Üô-di-lih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蘇西</rb><rt>Su-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈麥</rb><rt>Ha-mah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以米</rb><rt>Yi-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>沙微基列亭</rb><rt>Sô-vi-kyi-lih-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>何利</rb><rt>ʽO-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西珥</rb><rt>Si-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>珥巴蘭</rb><rt>R-pô-læn</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>走歸</rb><rt>tseo-kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安密八</rb><rt>En-mih-pah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>破進</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞瑪勒</rb><rt>Üô-mô-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哈洗遜大瑪</u>個</rb><rt>Ha-si-seng-da-mô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>蛾摩拉</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>押瑪</rb><rt>Iah-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>洗扁</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>比拉</rb><rt>Pi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>（<u><ruby><rb>比拉</rb><rt>Pi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑣珥</rb><rt>So-r</rt></ruby></u>）<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西定</rb><rt>Si-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山嶴</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以攔</rb><rt>Yi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基大老瑪</rb><rt>Kyi-da-lao-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>列邦</rb><rt>Lih-pông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提達</rb><rt>Di-dah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>士拿</rb><rt>Z-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>暗拉非</rb><rt>En-lah-fi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以拉撒</rb><rt>Yi-lah-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞略</rb><rt>Üô-liah</rt></ruby></u>：<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西定</rb><rt>Si-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山嶴</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>石油個</rb><rt>zah-yiu-go</rt><rb>地坑</rb><rt>di-kʽang</rt></ruby>：<u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蛾摩拉</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>打敗</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>逃到</rb><rt>dao-tao</rt><rb>山裏了</rb><rt>sæn-li-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>蛾摩拉</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>糧草</rb><rt>liang-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>擄掠仔</rb><rt>lo-liah-ts</rt><rb>走歸去</rb><rt>tseo-kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb><u>亞伯蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn-go</rt><rb>姪子</rb><rt>dzih-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u>：<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>擄掠去</rb><rt>lo-liah-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>逃走個</rb><rt>dao-tseo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>幔利</u>個</rb><rt>Mæn-li-go</rt><rb>橡樹</rb><rt>ziang-jü</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>幔利</rb><rt>Mæn-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實各</rb><rt>Yi-jih-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞乃</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-næ-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立約過了個</rb><rt>lih-iah-ko-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>姪子</rb><rt>dzih-ts</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>擄掠去</rb><rt>lo-liah-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>養東</rb><rt>iang-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>習練過了個</rb><rt>jih-lin-ko-liao-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>三百</rb><rt>sæn-pah</rt><rb>十八個</rb><rt>jih-pah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕到</rb><rt>ken-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>但</u>個</rb><rt>Dæn-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>連夜</rb><rt>Lin-yia</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打敗</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕到</rb><rt>ken-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>借手邊個</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何把</rb><rt>ʽO-pô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奪轉</rb><rt>deh-cün</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>姪子</rb><rt>dzih-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>殺敗了</rb><rt>sah-ba-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基大老瑪</rb><rt>Kyi-da-lao-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>結黨個</rb><rt>kyih-tông-go</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>沙微</rb><rt>Sô-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山嶴</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>王個</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>山嶴</rb><rt>sæn-ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>祝謝</rb><rt>Coh-zia</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>！」<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十股得一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-teh-ih</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>麥基洗德</rb><rt>Mah-kyi-si-teh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>馱勒去</rb><rt>do-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup>
