### Ts. XV.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>盾牌</rb><rt>deng-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>恩賞</rb><rt>eng-sông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>總管個</rb><rt>cong-kwun-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>生來</rb><rt>sang-læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>生出來個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>Deo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>數勒遍</rb><rt>su-leh-pin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦勒底</rb><rt>Kyüô-leh-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>珥</u>個</rb><rt>R-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>？」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>三年個</rb><rt>sæn-nyin-go</rt><rb>雌牛</rb><rt>tsʽ-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三年個</rb><rt>sæn-nyin-go</rt><rb>雌</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三年個</rb><rt>sæn-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>斑鳩</rb><rt>pæn-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>對</rb><rt>te</rt><rb>破開</rb><rt>pʽo-kʽæ</rt><rb>半邊</rb><rt>pun-pin</rt><rb>夾起</rb><rt>kah-kyʽi</rt><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>擺東</rb><rt>pa-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>破開</rb><rt>pʽo-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>野鳥</rb><rt>yia-tiao</rt><rb>飛落來</rb><rt>fi-loh-læ</rt><rb>停勒</rb><rt>ding-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牲口裏</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>loh-sæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>煞覺</rb><rt>sah-kao</rt><rb>睏熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>墨漆地暗</rb><rt>moh-tsʽeh-di-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怕了</rb><rt>pʽô-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>箇國個</rb><rt>keh-koh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>總要</rb><rt>tsong-iao</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>安安穩穩</rb><rt>en-en-w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>長長</rb><rt>dziang-dziang</rt><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>安葬好</rb><rt>en-tsông-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第四</rb><rt>di-s</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>loh-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>出煙個</rb><rt>cʽih-in-go</rt><rb>火爐</rb><rt>ho-lu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>猛猛</rb><rt>mang-mang</rt><rb>火把</rb><rt>ho-pô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>擺開的</rb><rt>pa-kʽæ-tih</rt><rb>肉個</rb><rt>nyüoh-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立約</rb><rt>lih-iah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯拉</rb><rt>Pah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>大河</rb><rt>do-ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基尼</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基尼洗</rb><rt>Kyi-nyi-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>甲摩尼</rb><rt>Kyiah-mo-nyi</rt></ruby></u>、<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利乏音</rb><rt>Li-vah-ing</rt></ruby></u>、<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>革迦撒</rb><rt>Keh-kyüô-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布西</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份個</rb><rt>dzoh-veng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Ts. XVI.

<sup>1</sup> <ruby><rb><u>亞伯蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>生養</rb><rt>sang-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>弗撥</rb><rt>feh-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>生養</rb><rt>sang-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>十</rb><rt>jih</rt><rb>年了</rb><rt>nyin-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿姨</rb><rt>ah-yi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有身</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看輕</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主母</rb><rt>cü-meo</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>加氣</rb><rt>kô-kyʽi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>懷裏</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有身</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看輕</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>Ô-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逃走了</rb><rt>dao-tseo-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>書珥</rb><rt>Shü-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>。）<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>撒萊</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-læ-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>主母</rb><rt>cü-meo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒萊</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-læ-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>主母</rb><rt>cü-meo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>甚許多</rb><rt>zing-hyü-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>數</rb><rt>su</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>數弗遍</rb><rt>su-feh-pin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>野</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆人個</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟個</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>東</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>啥弗是</rb><rt>soh-feh-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>見過</rb><rt>kyin-ko</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>東個</rb><rt>tong-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>箇口</rb><rt>keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別拉海萊</rb><rt>Bih-lah-hæ-læ</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>比列</rb><rt>Pi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Keh-we weh-go, we-kʽen-kyin-go Jing-ming go tsing. 就是箇位活個、會看見個神明個井。（原註）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>八十六</rb><rt>pah-jih-loh</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. XVII.

<u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>九十九</rb><rt>kyiu-jih-kyiu</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>全能個</rb><rt>djün-neng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>全備</rb><rt>djün-be</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>茂茂盛盛</rb><rt>meo-meo-zing-zing</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup>「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>列國個</rb><rt>lih-koh-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯蘭</rb><rt>Üô-pah-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>列國個</rb><rt>lih-koh-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歷代個</rb><rt>lih-dæ-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>後代個</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu z* Hyü-to nying-go tsu-tsong. 就是許多人個祖宗。（原註）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歷代個</rb><rt>lih-dæ-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>總要</rb><rt>tsong-iao</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>保守個</rb><rt>pao-siu-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>立約個</rb><rt>lih-iah-go</rt><rb>記號</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>歷代個</rb><rt>lih-dæ-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>八日個</rb><rt>pah-nyih-go</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>娃</rb><rt>w̆æn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>生來</rb><rt>sang-læ</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>買來個</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>Feh-leng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>生出個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>買來個</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮個</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li-go</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總要</rb><rt>tsong-iao</rt><rb>斷絕</rb><rt>dön-djih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] 割禮</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒萊</rb><rt>Sah-læ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福</rb><rt>foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福</rb><rt>foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>列國個</rb><rt>lih-koh-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>笑</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>一笑</rb><rt>ih-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>一百</rb><rt>Ih-pah</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>況且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>九十</rb><rt>kyiu-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>！」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗是介</rb><rt>Feh-z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福</rb><rt>foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>茂茂盛盛</rb><rt>meo-meo-zing-zing</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>明年</rb><rt>ming-nyin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>講好</rb><rt>Kông-hao</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>升上去</rb><rt>sing-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Sah-lah i-s ziu-z* Kong-cü. <u>撒拉</u>意思就是公主。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z* Fah-siao, kʽen <sup>17</sup> tsʽih. 就是發笑，看 <sup>17</sup> 七。（原註）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>生東個</rb><rt>sang-tong-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>買來個</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>九十九</rb><rt>kyiu-jih-kyiu</rt><rb>歲了</rb><rt>shü-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>十三</rb><rt>jih-sæn</rt><rb>歲了</rb><rt>shü-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一家個</rb><rt>ih-kô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>生來</rb><rt>sang-læ</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>買來個</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ts. XVIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>幔利</rb><rt>Mæn-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>橡</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>晝過</rb><rt>tsiu-ko</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>坐來</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一看見</rb><rt>ih-kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>奔上去</rb><rt>peng-zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>走開去</rb><rt>tseo-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>稍勢</rb><rt>siao-shü</rt><rb>馱點</rb><rt>do-tin</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>將息</rb><rt>tsiang-sih</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>馱點</rb><rt>do-tin</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>接接</rb><rt>tsih-tsih</rt><rb>力</rb><rt>lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>應當</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>帳篷裏</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>上緊</rb><rt>zông-kying</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>細阿</rb><rt>*si-ah*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>細細個</rb><rt>si-si-go</rt><rb>麪粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𢫭帶好</rb><rt>nyüoh-tæn-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>烹</rb><rt>pʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>奔到</rb><rt>peng-tao</rt><rb>牛圈裏</rb><rt>ngeo-gyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>壯壯</rb><rt>công-công</rt><rb>好好個</rb><rt>hao-hao-go</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奶油</rb><rt>na-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擺來</rb><rt>pa-læ</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] m-nao，現代寧波話說 mau6、mhau5，寫作「莫」，本字或爲「嘸得好」。</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ih si-ah da-iah* ih teo. 一細阿大約一斗。（原註）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>帳篷裏</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>月分</rb><rt>yüih-veng</rt><rb>足個</rb><rt>tsoh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>撒拉</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-lah-go</rt><rb>月經</rb><rt>yüih-kying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>斷了</rb><rt>dön-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>笑</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>一笑</rb><rt>ih-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>喜事</rb><rt>hyi-z</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>笑</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>做弗來</rb><rt>tso-feh-læ</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>定規個</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>月分</rb><rt>yüih-veng</rt><rb>足個</rb><rt>tsoh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>賴了</rb><rt>la-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>笑</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>笑過了</rb><rt>siao-ko-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>隱瞞</rb><rt>ing-mun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能幹個</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>應許個</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>蛾摩拉</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>惡貫</rb><rt>ôh-kwun</rt><rb>滿盈</rb><rt>mun-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>loh-kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>行爲果然</rb><rt>ʽang-we-ko-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>Liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] nyin-cün，現代寧波話說 gnieu tsoe 或 gniu tsoe，寫作「扭轉」，首字本字不明。</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>公義</rb><rt>Kong-yi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>一概</rb><rt>ih-kæ</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>Dzing-li</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五十個</rb><rt>ng-jih-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五十個</rb><rt>ng-jih-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>寬恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>公義</rb><rt>Kong-yi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt><rb>一概</rb><rt>ih-kæ</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>判斷</rb><rt>Pʽun-tön</rt><rb>天下個</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>公平</rb><rt>kong-bing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>五十個</rb><rt>ng-jih-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>寬恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>塳塵</rb><rt>bong-dzing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>灰</rb><rt>hwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>五十個</rb><rt>ng-jih-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>四十五個</rb><rt>s-jih-ng-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>廿個</rb><rt>nyiæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>廿個</rb><rt>nyiæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>走歸</rb><rt>tseo-kyü</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. XIX.

<ruby><rb>快夜</rb><rt>Kwʽa-yia</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>坐來</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt><rb>城門口</rb><rt>dzing-meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一看見</rb><rt>ih-kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>宿過</rb><rt>soh-ko</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗是介</rb><rt>Feh-z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>宿</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路街裏</rb><rt>lu-ka-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>煞扣</rb><rt>sah-kʽeo</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>一席</rb><rt>ih-zih</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>烹</rb><rt>pʽang</rt><rb>嘸酵水個</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>Wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>睏個</rb><rt>kwʽeng-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>四凸亂圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>少少</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>圍住</rb><rt>we-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>關好</rb><rt>kwæn-hao</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>作惡</rb><rt>tsoh-ôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>難爲</rb><rt>næn-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>擁擠</rb><rt>üong-tsi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>破開</rb><rt>pʽo-kʽæ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>拉</rb><rt>l̆a</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>關門</rb><rt>kwæn-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>門外個</rb><rt>meng-nga-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>少少</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>瞎了</rb><rt>hah-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫喫</rb><rt>kyʽih-kyʽih</rt><rb>力力</rb><rt>lih-lih</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>女婿</rb><rt>nyü-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>帶其出</rb><rt>ta-gyi-cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇勒</rb><rt>keh-leh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>囡個</rb><rt>nön-go</rt><rb>女婿</rb><rt>nyü-si</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>起身</rb><rt>kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>女婿</rb><rt>nyü-si</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>謔言</rb><rt>hyiah-yin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>亮起</rb><rt>liang-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>催</rb><rt>tsʽe</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>起身</rb><rt>kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>妻子等</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts-teng</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>城裏個</rb><rt>dzing-li-go</rt><rb>惡逆</rb><rt>ôh-nyih</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>捱捱棲棲</rb><rt>nga-nga-tsʽi-tsʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拉勒</rb><rt>l̆a-leh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>囡個</rb><rt>nön-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>dzing-nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>既經</rb><rt>Kyi-kying</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>平垟裏</rb><rt>bing-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>逃到</rb><rt>dao-tao</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>大慈</rb><rt>da-dz</rt><rb>大悲</rb><rt>da-pe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>逃到</rb><rt>dao-tao</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>近</rb><rt>gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt><rb>逃勒</rb><rt>dao-leh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>豈弗是</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-z</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？）<ruby><rb>何弗</rb><rt>ʽo-feh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>逃到</rb><rt>dao-tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>逃到</rb><rt>dao-tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗來</rb><rt>feh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑣珥</rb><rt>So-r</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。」

<sup>\[1\]*Ziu-z* Siao-go, kʽen <sup>20</sup> tsʽih. 就是小個，看 <sup>20</sup> 七。（原註）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑣珥</rb><rt>So-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>出起</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djoh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蛾摩拉</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>降落來個</rb><rt>kông-loh-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>地土個</rb><rt>di-tʽu-go</rt><rb>出產</rb><rt>cʽih-tsʽæn</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>除滅了</rb><rt>djü-mih-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb><u>羅得</u>個</rb><rt>Lo-teh-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>鹽個</rb><rt>yin-go</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>立起</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前個</rb><rt>min-zin-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>Dziao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>蛾摩拉</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt><rb>衝上</rb><rt>cʽong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火盧裏</rb><rt>ho-lu-li</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>平垟裏</rb><rt>bing-yiang-li</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>滅亡個</rb><rt>mih-vông-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑣珥</rb><rt>So-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑣珥</rb><rt>So-r</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山巖裏</rb><rt>sæn-ngæn-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>大囡</rb><rt>Do-nön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小囡</rb><rt>siao-nön</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>鄉方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>喫酒</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>傳宗接代</rb><rt>djün-cong-tsih-dæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>喫酒</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大囡</rb><rt>do-nön</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>大囡</rb><rt>do-nön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小囡</rb><rt>siao-nön</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>昨日</rb><rt>zô-nyih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>喫酒</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>傳宗接代</rb><rt>djün-cong-tsih-dæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>喫酒</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>小囡</rb><rt>siao-nön</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>大囡</rb><rt>Do-nön</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩押</rb><rt>Mo-iah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族個</rb><rt>keh-dzoh-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>小囡</rb><rt>Siao-nön</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便亞米</rb><rt>Bin-üô-mi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞捫</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族個</rb><rt>keh-dzoh-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。
