### Ts. XX.

<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>搬到</rb><rt>pun-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>南</rb><rt>Nen</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦鐵</rb><rt>Kyüô-tʽih</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>書珥</u>個</rb><rt>Shü-r-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其臘</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿妹</rb><rt>ah-me</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其臘</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>夢裏</rb><rt>mong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>領着</rb><rt>ling-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>取個</rb><rt>cʽü-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>近身</rb><rt>gying-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿妹</rb><rt>ah-me</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>端正</rb><rt>tön-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>潔淨個</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>夢裏</rb><rt>mong-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>Z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>端正個</rb><rt>tön-tsing-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>近身</rb><rt>gying-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>歸還</rb><rt>kwe-wæn</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>一國</rb><rt>ih-koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>見究</rb><rt>kyin-kyiu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>阿妹</rb><rt>ah-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>搬來搬去個</rb><rt>pun-læ-pun-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>塊</rb><rt>kwʽe</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>同隊個</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>遮</rb><rt>tsô</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>責罰</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生養</rb><rt>sang-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒拉</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-lah-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>養了</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. XXI.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>照顧</rb><rt>tsiao-kwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>應許個</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有身</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定個</rb><rt>ding-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>生落</rb><rt>Sang-loh</rt><rb>第八</rb><rt>di-pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>歲了</rb><rt>shü-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>發笑</rb><rt>fah-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>笑</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>摘奶了</rb><rt>tsah-na-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>摘奶個</rb><rt>tsah-na-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>大菜</rb><rt>do-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>嬉笑</rb><rt>hyi-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>趕出</rb><rt>Ken-cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>丫頭個</rb><rt>ô-deo-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>以<u>撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt><rb>大</u>家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>接代</rb><rt>tsih-dæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>丫頭個</rb><rt>ô-deo-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>：<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>丫頭個</rb><rt>ô-deo-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>立起</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>肩胛頭</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別是巴</rb><rt>Bih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野裏</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>袋裏個</rb><rt>Dæ-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>用完了</rb><rt>yüong-wun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>走過去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>一箭</rb><rt>ih-tsin</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>坐帶落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗捨得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>哭起來了</rb><rt>kʽoh-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>小人個</rb><rt>siao-nying-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>擋勒</rb><rt>tông-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一口</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水個</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>𣥼滿</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>保佑</rb><rt>pao-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>射箭</rb><rt>zih-tsin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野裏</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非各</rb><rt>Fi-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>保佑</rb><rt>pao-yiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>欺待</rb><rt>kyʽi-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>孫子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>耽擱個</rb><rt>tæn-koh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>兩下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立約</rb><rt>lih-iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雌個</rb><rt>tsʽ-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雌個</rb><rt>tsʽ-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>？」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七隻</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsah</rt><rb>雌個</rb><rt>tsʽ-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>箇口</rb><rt>keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別是巴</rb><rt>Bih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> ### Ts. XXII.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>！」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>Ta-leh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>值鈿個</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt><rb>獨養</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩利</rb><rt>Mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>早天亮</rb><rt>tsao-tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>起身</rb><rt>kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牽勒</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>劈勒</rb><rt>pʽih-leh</rt><rb>獻祭個</rb><rt>hyin-tsi-go</rt><rb>柴</rb><rt>za</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>等東</rb><rt>teng-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt><rb>獻祭個</rb><rt>hyin-tsi-go</rt><rb>柴</rb><rt>za</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>柴</rb><rt>za</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>獻祭個</rb><rt>hyin-tsi-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就是介</rb><rt>ziu-z-ka</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>Tseo-tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指點個</rb><rt>ts-tin-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>柴</rb><rt>za</rt><rb>疊好仔</rb><rt>diah-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>縛勒</rb><rt>bo-leh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>安來</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>壇裏個</rb><rt>dæn-li-go</rt><rb>柴</rb><rt>za</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>！」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>小人個</rb><rt>siao-nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>獨養</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>弗捨得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>樹縫裏</rb><rt>jü-vong-li</rt><rb>軋住的</rb><rt>gah-djü-tih</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲勒</rb><rt>kʽô-leh</rt><rb>箇隻</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>葉徠</rb><rt>yih-læ</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>」。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>備好了個</rb><rt>be-hao-liao-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>弗捨得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>獨養</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>Pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>茂茂盛盛</rb><rt>meo-meo-zing-zing</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>海塗個</rb><rt>hæ-du-go</rt><rb>沙泥</rb><rt>sô-nyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>仇敵個</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>城門</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>走歸</rb><rt>tseo-kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別是巴</rb><rt>Bih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別是巴</rb><rt>Bih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿鶴</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>戶斯</rb><rt>Wu-s</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>布斯</rb><rt>Pu-s</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基母利</rb><rt>Kyi-meo-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞蘭</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-læn-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基薛</rb><rt>Kyi-sih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈瑣</rb><rt>Ha-so</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>必達</rb><rt>Pih-dah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>益拉</rb><rt>Ih-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>彼土利</rb><rt>Pe-tʽu-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼土利</rb><rt>Pe-tʽu-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>八個</rb><rt>pah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿鶴</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb><u>拿鶴</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh-go</rt><rb>阿姨</rb><rt>ah-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流瑪</rb><rt>Liu-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提八</rb><rt>Di-pah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>迦含</rb><rt>Kyüô-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>大轄</rb><rt>Da-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>瑪迦</rb><rt>Mô-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。」

---

### Ts. XXIII.

<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>廿七</rb><rt>nyiæn-tsʽih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>活東個</rb><rt>weh-tong-go</rt><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死來</rb><rt>si-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列亞巴</rb><rt>Kyi-lih-üô-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯崙</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>哀痛</rb><rt>æ-tʽong</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死人個</rb><rt>si-nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup>「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做墳個</rb><rt>tso-veng-go</rt><rb>地基</rb><rt>di-kyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>6</sup>「<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>珍重</rb><rt>tsing-djong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>墳地</rb><rt>veng-di</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>揀擇</rb><rt>kæn-dzeh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>箇族</rb><rt>keh-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>傴帶倒</rb><rt>eo-tæn-tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>瑣轄</u>個</rb><rt>So-ʽeh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗崙</rb><rt>Yi-feh-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>田角裏</rb><rt>din-koh-li</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>麥比拉</u>個</rb><rt>Mah-pi-lah-go</rt><rb>穴</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>足足</rb><rt>tsoh-tsoh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>墳個</rb><rt>veng-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以弗崙</rb><rt>Yi-feh-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>坐的</rb><rt>zo-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>赫</u>人</rb><rt>Heh-nying</rt><rb>箇族</rb><rt>keh-dzoh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>城門</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聽東</rb><rt>tʽing-tong</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗崙</rb><rt>Yi-feh-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>11</sup>「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>穴</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>傴帶倒</rb><rt>eo-tæn-tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗崙</rb><rt>Yi-feh-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>肯個</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>田價</rb><rt>din-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>接受仔</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以弗崙</rb><rt>Yi-feh-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>15</sup>「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>值</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>成功</rb><rt>dzing-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>瓶</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾自</rb><rt>Ng-zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打水</rb><rt>tang-shü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>定好</rb><rt>ding-hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] 賜福；保佑</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>Shih-wô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>肩胛頭</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt><rb>瓶</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拿鶴</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>密迦</u>個</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼土利</rb><rt>Pe-tʽu-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>童女</rb><rt>dong-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>人過</rb><rt>nying-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt><rb>井裏</rb><rt>tsing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瓶</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>打滿仔</rb><rt>tang-mun-ts</rt><rb>走上來</rb><rt>tseo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>家人</rb><rt>Kô-nying</rt><rb>奔攏去</rb><rt>peng-long-kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>馱點</rb><rt>do-tin</rt><rb>瓶裏個</rb><rt>bing-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>瓶</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫好仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打水</rb><rt>tang-shü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>爽快</rb><rt>shông-kwʽa</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>瓶裏個</rb><rt>bing-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>倒來</rb><rt>tao-læ</rt><rb>槽裏</rb><rt>zao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>奔到</rb><rt>peng-tao</rt><rb>井裏</rb><rt>tsing-li</rt><rb>打水</rb><rt>tang-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>打個</rb><rt>tang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗聲</rb><rt>feh-sing</rt><rb>弗響</rb><rt>feh-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一路</rb><rt>ih-lu</rt><rb>順流</rb><rt>jing-liu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>Keh-dziao</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>喫好仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>鼻圈</rb><rt>bih-kyʽün</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>手鐲</rb><rt>siu-gyüoh</rt><rb>一對</rb><rt>ih-te</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>空屋</rb><rt>kʽong-oh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>庉宿</rb><rt>deng-soh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>童女</rb><rt>Dong-nyü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>密迦</rb><rt>Mih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生撥</rb><rt>sang-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拿鶴</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>彼土利</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-tʽu-li-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>草料</rb><rt>tsʽao-liao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>庉宿個</rb><rt>deng-soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>多謝</rb><rt>To-zia</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>錯落</rb><rt>tsʽo-loh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>誠實</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>兄弟個</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>童女</rb><rt>dong-nyü</rt><rb>奔起</rb><rt>peng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>奔起</rb><rt>peng-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>鼻圈</rb><rt>bih-kyʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>鐲子</rb><rt>gyüoh-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>姊妹個</rb><rt>tsi-me-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜福個</rb><rt>s-foh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>走進來</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>解勒</rb><rt>ka-leh</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>揹個</rb><rt>pe-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>草料</rb><rt>tsʽao-liao</rt><rb>餵</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>跟從</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>擺</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>來意</rb><rt>læ-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>家人</rb><rt>kô-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>家人</rb><rt>kô-nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>家人</rb><rt>Kô-nying</rt><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>金銀個</rb><rt>kying-nying-go</rt><rb>首飾</rb><rt>siu-sih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>寶貝</rb><rt>pao-pe</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>喫喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>宿過</rb><rt>soh-ko</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>55</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>幾日</rb><rt>kyi-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>少</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>十日</rb><rt>jih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>順流</rb><rt>jing-liu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問問</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>情願個</rb><rt>Dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿哺</rb><rt>ah-bu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>家人</rb><rt>kô-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>60</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>千萬</rb><rt>tsʽin-væn</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>仇敵個</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>城門</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>61</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>Ô-deo</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>騎勒</rb><rt>gyi-leh</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>家人</rb><rt>kô-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>家人</rb><rt>kô-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>62</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>Nen-pin</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別拉海萊</rb><rt>Bih-lah-hæ-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>63</sup> <ruby><rb>夜快</rb><rt>Yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>思忖</rb><rt>s-tsʽeng</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>64</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-loh</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt></ruby>。<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>家人</rb><rt>kô-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>田裏</rb><rt>Din-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>家人</rb><rt>Kô-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>手帕</rb><rt>siu-pʽao</rt><rb>遮</rb><rt>tsô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>66</sup> <ruby><rb>家人</rb><rt>Kô-nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>67</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒拉</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-lah-go</rt><rb>帳篷裏</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>安慰了</rb><rt>en-w̆e-de</rt></ruby>。
