### Ts. XXV.

<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基都拉</rb><rt>Kyi-tu-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>心蘭</rb><rt>Sing-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>約珊</rb><rt>Iah-sæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>米但</rb><rt>Mi-dæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>益八</rb><rt>Ih-pah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>書亞</rb><rt>Shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約珊</rb><rt>Iah-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示巴</rb><rt>Z-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底但</rb><rt>Ti-dæn</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>底但</u>個</rb><rt>Ti-dæn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞書利</rb><rt>Üô-shü-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利都是</rb><rt>Li-tu-z</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利烏米</rb><rt>Li-u-mi</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法</rb><rt>Yi-fah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以弗</rb><rt>Yi-feh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈諾</rb><rt>Ha-noh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞比大</rb><rt>Üô-pi-da</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以拉大</rb><rt>Yi-lah-da</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基都拉</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-tu-lah-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>活東個</rb><rt>weh-tong-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>阿姨個</rb><rt>ah-yi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>東半邊</rb><rt>tong-pun-pin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>東邊個</rb><rt>tong-pin-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>活東個</rb><rt>weh-tong-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>七十五</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-ng</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有福</rb><rt>yiu-foh</rt><rb>有壽</rb><rt>yiu-ziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>斷氣</rb><rt>dön-kyʽi</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>幔利</rb><rt>Mæn-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>對面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>麥比拉</u>個</rb><rt>Mah-pi-lah-go</rt><rb>穴</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>瑣轄</u>個</rb><rt>So-ʽeh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以弗崙</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-feh-leng-go</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>買個</rb><rt>ma-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>葬間</rb><rt>tsông-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別拉海萊</rb><rt>Bih-lah-hæ-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb><u>撒拉</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-lah-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>夏甲</rb><rt>Yüô-kyiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生撥</rb><rt>sang-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：<u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼徘藥</rb><rt>Nyi-bæ-yiah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基達</rb><rt>Kyi-dah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>押別</rb><rt>Iah-bih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>密衫</rb><rt>Mih-sæn</rt></ruby></u>、<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>密挪</rb><rt>Mih-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>土姆</rb><rt>Tʽu-m</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>瑪撒</rb><rt>Mô-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哈達</rb><rt>Ha-dah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>提瑪</rb><rt>Di-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>益督</rb><rt>Ih-toh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拿非實</rb><rt>Nô-fi-jih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>其底瑪</rb><rt>Gyi-ti-mô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以實瑪利</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>寨基</rb><rt>za-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以實瑪利</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li-go</rt><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十七</rb><rt>sæn-jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斷氣</rb><rt>dön-kyʽi</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>族種</rb><rt>dzoh-cong</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈腓拉</rb><rt>Ha-fi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>書珥</rb><rt>Shü-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞述</rb><rt>Üô-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟個</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴坦亞蘭</rb><rt>Pô-tʽæn-üô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞蘭</rb><rt>Üô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>彼土利</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-tʽu-li-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞蘭</rb><rt>Üô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拉班</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-pæn-go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>受胎</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>胎</rb><rt>Tʽæ</rt><rb>裏向個</rb><rt>li-hyiang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>爭造</rb><rt>tsang-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>胎裏</rb><rt>tʽæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩樣</rb><rt>liang-yiang</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>分出來</rb><rt>feng-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>強如</rb><rt>gyiang-jü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>小個</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>生產</rb><rt>sang-tsʽæn</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>胎裏</rb><rt>tʽæ-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>雙生</rb><rt>shông-sang</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>Deo</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>咸肌身</rb><rt>ʽen-kyi-sing</rt><rb>紅紅個</rb><rt>ʽong-ʽong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>皮襖</rb><rt>bi-ao</rt><rb>穿的</rb><rt>cʽün-tih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以掃</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sao-go</rt><rb>腳後跟</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽeo-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>田獵</rb><rt>din-liah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>帳篷裏</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>樸實</rb><rt>pʽoh-jih</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鹿</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* ʽOng go. 就是紅個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z* S-en-shün. 就是使暗算。（原註）</sup>

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>煮</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>羹</rb><rt>kang</rt></ruby>：<u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫力了</rb><rt>kyʽih-lih-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>喫力了</rb><rt>kyʽih-lih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>紅個</rb><rt>ʽong-go</rt><rb>羹</rb><rt>kang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>長子個</rb><rt>tsiang-ts-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>賣撥</rb><rt>ma-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>長子個</rb><rt>tsiang-ts-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>於</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>對</rb><rt>te</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>長子個</rb><rt>tsiang-ts-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>賣撥</rb><rt>ma-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>紅豆羹</rb><rt>ʽong-deo-kang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>喫喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>輕看</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>長子個</rb><rt>tsiang-ts-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* ʽOng go. 就是紅個。（原註）</sup>

### Ts. XXVI.

<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其臘</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>制度</rb><rt>cü-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其臘</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>問起</rb><rt>meng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>弗敢</rb><rt>feh-ken</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>檻頭</rb><rt>kʽæn-deo</rt><rb>望出去</rb><rt>mông-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>嬉戲</rb><rt>hyi-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喪命</rb><rt>sông-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘸規</rb><rt>m-kwe</rt><rb>嘸矩</rb><rt>m-kyü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子個</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>耕種</rb><rt>kang-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當年</rb><rt>tông-nyin</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>整日</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逐節</rb><rt>djoh-tsih</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>有佬了</rb><rt>yiu-lao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>隊數</rb><rt>de-su</rt><rb>牛羊</rb><rt>ngeo-yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>盛多少</rb><rt>zing-to-siao</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗過</rb><rt>feh-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>掘間個</rb><rt>gyüih-kæn-go</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>塞塞</rb><rt>seh-seh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>填滿</rb><rt>din-mun</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>其臘</u>個</rb><rt>Gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>山嶴裏</rb><rt>sæn-ao-li</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>掘間個</rb><rt>gyüih-kæn-go</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>塞塞個</rb><rt>seh-seh-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>謳個</rb><rt>eo-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山嶴裏</rb><rt>sæn-ao-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爭造</rb><rt>tsang-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爭造</rb><rt>tsang-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇口</rb><rt>keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃色</rb><rt>Yiæ-seh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>一口</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爭造</rb><rt>tsang-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>薛拿</rb><rt>Sih-nô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>一口</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>爭造</rb><rt>tsang-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利河伯</rb><rt>Li-ʽo-pah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>寬空</rb><rt>kwʽun-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>興旺起來</rb><rt>hying-wông-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別是巴</rb><rt>Bih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇夜</rb><rt>Keh-yia</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗用</rb><rt>feh-yüong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z* Ün-kyʽi. 就是怨氣。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[3\] *Ziu-z* Kwʽun-kʽong. 就是寬空。（原註）</sup>

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞比米力</rb><rt>Üô-pi-mi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞戶撒</rb><rt>Üô-wu-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非各</rb><rt>Fi-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>其臘</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>離開了</rb><rt>li-kʽæ-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立約</rb><rt>lih-iah</rt></ruby>。』<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>安安耽耽</rb><rt>en-en-tæn-tæn</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>福氣了</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>辦酒</rb><rt>bæn-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>喫喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>安安耽耽</rb><rt>en-en-tæn-tæn</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>通報</rb><rt>tʽong-pao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掘井個</rb><rt>gyüih-tsing-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>水了</rb><rt>shü-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示巴</rb><rt>Z-pô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別是巴</rb><rt>Bih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Vah-tsiu. 就是罰咒。（原註）</sup>

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>別利</u>個</rb><rt>Bih-li-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶滴</rb><rt>Yiu-tih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以倫</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-leng-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴是抹</rb><rt>Pô-z-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. XXVII.

<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>花了</rb><rt>hwô-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>弓</rb><rt>kong</rt><rb>箭</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>鹿</rb><rt>loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲勒</rb><rt>kʽô-leh</rt><rb>鹿</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>7</sup> 『<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>鹿</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好喫個</rb><rt>hao-kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>喫仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>羊隊裏</rb><rt>yiang-de-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>好好個</rb><rt>hao-hao-go</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>馱去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>喫仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出毛個</rb><rt>cʽih-mao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>光滑個</rb><rt>kwông-wah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>號淚大哭</rb><rt>ʽao-li-da-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>！」<sup>35</sup> </sup><ruby><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩遭</rb><rt>liang-tsao</rt><rb>佔</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>位</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>奪</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>長子個</rb><rt>tsiang-ts-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>奪了去了</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>平落了</rb><rt>bing-loh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忘記</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋好</rb><rt>dza-hao</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>喪掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利百加</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>厭煩了</rb><rt>in-væn-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>此地個</rb><rt>tsʽ-di-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

### Ts. XXVIII.

<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴坦亞蘭</rb><rt>Pô-tʽæn-üô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>外公</rb><rt>nga-kong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>彼土利</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-tʽu-li-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>孃舅</rb><rt>nyiang-gyiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拉班</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-pæn-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>全能個</rb><rt>djün-neng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>茂茂盛盛</rb><rt>meo-meo-zing-zing</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>論千論萬個</rb><rt>leng-tsʽin-leng-væn-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>耽擱東個</rb><rt>tæn-koh-tong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴坦亞蘭</rb><rt>Pô-tʽæn-üô-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞蘭</rb><rt>Üô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>彼土利</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-tʽu-li-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>利百加</u>個</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴坦亞蘭</rb><rt>Pô-tʽæn-üô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>祝福個</rb><rt>coh-foh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>依了</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>爹孃</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴坦亞蘭</rb><rt>Pô-tʽæn-üô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>路梯</rb><rt>lu-tʽæ</rt><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djoh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天個</rb><rt>tʽin-go</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>本來</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路斯</rb><rt>Lu-s</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>願心</rb><rt>nyün-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保佑</rb><rt>pao-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>走個</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>衣穿</rb><rt>i-cʽün</rt></ruby>，<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>安安穩穩</rb><rt>en-en-w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>立個</rb><rt>lih-go</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt><rb>得一</rb><rt>teh-ih</rt><rb>獻撥</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」

### Ts. XXIX.

<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>一口</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三隊</rb><rt>sæn-de</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>睏間</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇口</rb><rt>keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>喫水</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>蓋</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>井口個</rb><rt>tsing-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>羊隊</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt><rb>聚攏來了</rb><rt>jü-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>發過</rb><rt>fah-ko</rt><rb>井口裏個</rb><rt>tsing-kʽeo-li-go</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>喫水</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>井口</rb><rt>tsing-kʽeo</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>蓋好</rb><rt>kæ-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈蘭</rb><rt>Ha-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拿鶴</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>認得</rb><rt>Nying-teh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好個</rb><rt>Hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>趕勒</rb><rt>ken-leh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>高高個</rb><rt>kao-kao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>衆牲</rb><rt>cong-sang</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何弗</rb><rt>ʽo-feh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-leh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>餵餵</rb><rt>ü-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弄弗來個</rb><rt>Long-feh-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>羊隊</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>發過</rb><rt>fah-ko</rt><rb>井口裏個</rb><rt>tsing-kʽeo-li-go</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>趕勒</rb><rt>ken-leh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>管個</rb><rt>kwun-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>孃舅</rb><rt>nyiang-gyiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拉班</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-pæn-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>孃舅</rb><rt>nyiang-gyiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拉班</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-pæn-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>發過</rb><rt>fah-ko</rt><rb>井口裏個</rb><rt>tsing-kʽeo-li-go</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>孃舅</rb><rt>nyiang-gyiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拉班</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-pæn-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>親嘴</rb><rt>tsʽing-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>哭起來了</rb><rt>kʽoh-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>外甥</rb><rt>nga-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>利百加</u>個</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奔去</rb><rt>peng-kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>外甥</rb><rt>nga-sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奔出</rb><rt>peng-cʽih</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>親嘴</rb><rt>tsʽing-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>骨頭</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>外甥</rb><rt>nga-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>大囡</rb><rt>do-nön</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>小囡</rb><rt>siao-nön</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>毛病</rb><rt>mao-bing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt><rb>裝滿</rb><rt>tsông-mun</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>小囡</rb><rt>siao-nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>七年</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>Pô</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>許撥</rb><rt>hyü-peh</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>許撥</rb><rt>hyü-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉東</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>七年</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>幾日</rb><rt>kyi-nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>Yia-tao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大囡</rb><rt>do-nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領去</rb><rt>ling-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>悉帕</rb><rt>Sih-pʽô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>豈弗是</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>哄騙</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>Feh-zing</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>小個</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做弗來個</rb><rt>tso-feh-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>七日個</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>七年</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>七年個</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyin-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>過如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>七年</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生養</rb><rt>sang-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>記念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>有身</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西緬</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有身</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>總會</rb><rt>tsong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>膠黏</rb><rt>kao-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>有身</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>生了</rb><rt>sang-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>割</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>麥個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>萱草</rb><rt>hyün-tsʽao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>萱草</rb><rt>hyün-tsʽao</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>分點</rb><rt>feng-tin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>佔住勒</rb><rt>tsin-djü-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>萱草</rb><rt>hyün-tsʽao</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>萱草</rb><rt>hyün-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>宿過</rb><rt>soh-ko</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>夜快</rb><rt>Yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>萱草</rb><rt>hyün-tsʽao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>定鈿</rb><rt>ding-din</rt><rb>僱</rb><rt>kwu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有身</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>第五個</rb><rt>di-ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以薩迦</rb><rt>Yi-sah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有身</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>第六個</rb><rt>di-loh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>嫁妝</rb><rt>kô-tsông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>總會</rb><rt>tsong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>六個</rb><rt>loh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西布倫</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>21</sup> </sup>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>記念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生養</rb><rt>sang-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>揩掉</rb><rt>kʽa-diao</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>湊</rb><rt>tsʽeo</rt></ruby>。」
