<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>情節</rb><rt>dzing-tsih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗多</rb><rt>feh-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>成家立業</rb><rt>dzing-kô-lih-nyih</rt></ruby>？」<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>餵</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>今末仔</rb><rt>Kyih-mih-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>點子</rb><rt>tin-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>瘢疤個</rb><rt>pæn-pô-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>棕色個</rb><rt>cong-seh-go</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>瘢疤</rb><rt>pæn-pô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>點子個</rb><rt>tin-ts-go</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>下遭</rb><rt>ʽô-tsao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>嘸點子</rb><rt>m-tin-ts</rt><rb>嘸瘢疤個</rb><rt>m-pæn-pô-go</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>棕色個</rb><rt>cong-seh-go</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>紋路</rb><rt>veng-lu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>瘢疤個</rb><rt>pæn-pô-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>點子</rb><rt>tin-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>瘢疤</rb><rt>pæn-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>花白個</rb><rt>hwô-bah-go</rt><rb>雌山羊</rb><rt>tsʽ-sæn-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>棕色個</rb><rt>cong-seh-go</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>三日個</rb><rt>sæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>仍舊</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>餵</rb><rt>ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>剩落東個</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong-go</rt><rb>羊隊</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>楊柳</rb><rt>yiang-liu</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>楓樹</rb><rt>fong-jü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>杏樹個</rb><rt>ʽang-jü-go</rt><rb>嫩</rb><rt>neng</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>剝掉</rb><rt>poh-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>皮</rb><rt>bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>紋路</rb><rt>veng-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>裏向個</rb><rt>li-hyiang-go</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>露出來</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>面色</rb><rt>min-seh</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>！』<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。』<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>伏</rb><rt>boh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>雌羊</rb><rt>tsʽ-yiang</rt><rb>上頂個</rb><rt>zông-teng-go</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>紋路</rb><rt>veng-lu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>點子</rb><rt>tin-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>花白個</rb><rt>hwô-bah-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>伯特利</u>個</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>願心個</rb><rt>nyün-sing-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>族種個</rb><rt>dzoh-cong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>分開個</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ-go</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遺落個</rb><rt>yi-loh-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>記掛</rb><rt>kyi-kwô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神像</rb><rt>jing-ziang</rt></ruby>？」<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神像</rb><rt>jing-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗曉得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>偷的了</rb><rt>tʽeo-tih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>利亞</u>個</rb><rt>Li-üô-go</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>利亞</u>個</rb><rt>Li-üô-go</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拉結</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-kyih-go</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>高山堆</rb><rt>kao-sæn-te</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>中央個</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>高山堆</rb><rt>kao-sæn-te</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>高山堆</rb><rt>kao-sæn-te</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>高山堆</rb><rt>kao-sæn-te</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>拿鶴</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-ngoh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>Pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>獻祭</rb><rt>hyin-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>喫餅</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>喫仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>宿過夜</rb><rt>soh-ko-yia</rt></ruby>。<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>親親</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ts. XXXII.

<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兵將</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>瑪哈念</rb><rt>Mô-ha-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西珥</rb><rt>Si-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以東</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-tong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Ping-de. 就是兵隊。（原註）</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>差去個</rb><rt>Tsʽa-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>四百個</rb><rt>s-pah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>爾了</rb><rt>ng-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>慌慌</rb><rt>hwông-hwông</rt><rb>忙忙</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>隊</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一隊</rb><rt>ih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>剩落東</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一隊</rb><rt>ih-de</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>避過</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>爺爺</rb><rt>yia-yia</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話過</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾自個</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>族種</rb><rt>dzoh-cong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>誠實</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>恩寵</rb><rt>eng-cʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一眼眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn-ngæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>當弗起</rb><rt>tông-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>一梗</rb><rt>ih-kwang</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇兩</rb><rt>keh-liang</rt><rb>隊</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>阿哥個</rb><rt>ah-ko-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以掃</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sao-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>海裏個</rb><rt>hæ-li-go</rt><rb>沙泥</rb><rt>sô-nyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>數</rb><rt>su</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>數弗</rb><rt>su-feh</rt><rb>遍</rb><rt>pin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>宿過</rb><rt>soh-ko</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>雌山羊</rb><rt>tsʽ-sæn-yiang</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄山羊</rb><rt>yüong-sæn-yiang</rt><rb>廿只</rb><rt>nyiæn-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雌湖羊</rb><rt>tsʽ-wu-yiang</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄湖羊</rb><rt>yüong-wu-yiang</rt><rb>廿只</rb><rt>nyiæn-tsah</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>奶個</rb><rt>na-go</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雌牛</rb><rt>tsʽ-ngeo</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>十隻</rb><rt>jih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雌驢子</rb><rt>tsʽ-li-ts</rt><rb>廿只</rb><rt>nyiæn-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>十隻</rb><rt>jih-tsah</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>Sô</rt><rb>分個</rb><rt>feng-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>隊數</rb><rt>de-su</rt><rb>樣打樣</rb><rt>yiang-tang-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>隊打隊</rb><rt>de-tang-de</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>空</rb><rt>kʽong</rt><rb>一彖</rb><rt>ih-tʽön</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>上前個</rb><rt>zông-zin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以掃</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>第二個</rb><rt>di-nyi-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>第三個</rb><rt>di-sæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>隊打隊</rb><rt>de-tang-de</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>。』」<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>送去</rb><rt>song-kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講和</rb><rt>kông-ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>見面</rb><rt>kyin-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>禮物</rb><rt>Li-veh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>送去</rb><rt>song-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>宿</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>Yia-tao</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>十一個</rb><rt>jih-ih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>蹽過</rb><rt>liao-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅泊</rb><rt>Yüô-boh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>渡</rb><rt>du</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>蹽過</rb><rt>liao-ko</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>溪坑</rb><rt>kyʽi-kʽang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>蹽</rb><rt>liao</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>獨自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>剩落東</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>比武</rb><rt>pi-vu</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>快亮</rb><rt>kwʽa-liang</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>捏</rb><rt>nyiah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大腿個</rb><rt>do-tʽe-go</rt><rb>絞股</rb><rt>gao-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>比武個</rb><rt>pi-vu-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大腿</rb><rt>do-tʽe</rt><rb>絞股</rb><rt>gao-kwu</rt><rb>脫穴</rb><rt>tʽeh-yüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>亮了</rb><rt>liang-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>因爲爾</rb><rt>ing-we-ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>武</rb><rt>vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得勝了</rb><rt>teh-sing-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>便以利</rb><rt>Bin-yi-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>對面</rb><rt>te-min</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>保全了</rb><rt>pao-djün-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便以利</rb><rt>Bin-yi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>大腿</rb><rt>do-tʽe</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拐腳個</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>捏</rb><rt>nyiah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大腿</rb><rt>do-tʽe</rt><rb>絞股裏個</rb><rt>gao-kwu-li-go</rt><rb>筋</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>大腿</rb><rt>do-tʽe</rt><rb>絞股裏個</rb><rt>gao-kwu-li-go</rt><rb>筋</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Teng Jing-ming pi-wu-go. ʽÔh-tsia Jing-ming pi-vu. 就是等神明比武個。或者神明比武。（原註）</sup>

### Ts. XXXIII.

<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>四百個</rb><rt>s-pah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>七遭</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>奔攏來</rb><rt>peng-long-læ</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捼勒</rb><rt>o-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭頸</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>親</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>出了</rb><rt>cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>伏帶倒</rb><rt>boh-tæn-tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>伏帶倒</rb><rt>boh-tæn-tao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>伏帶倒</rb><rt>boh-tæn-tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着個</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>隊數</rb><rt>de-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>cü-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>夠了</rb><rt>keo-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有餘</rb><rt>yiu-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>催</rb><rt>tsʽe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>收了</rb><rt>siu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>引導</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>單薄</rb><rt>tæn-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>受胎個</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>東</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一大隊</rb><rt>ih-do-de</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>前頭個</rb><rt>zin-deo-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走個</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>腳步</rb><rt>kyiah-bu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>cü-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西珥</rb><rt>Si-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同隊個</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>何</rb><rt>ʽO</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>cü-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西珥</rb><rt>Si-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>疏割</rb><rt>Su-keh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>搭棚</rb><rt>tah-bang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>疏割</rb><rt>Su-keh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Bang. 就是棚。（原註）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴坦亞蘭</rb><rt>Pô-tʽæn-üô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>安安耽耽</rb><rt>en-en-tæn-tæn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>示劍</u>個</rb><rt>Z-kyin-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>塊</rb><rt>kwʽe</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>示劍</u>個</rb><rt>Z-kyin-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈抹</rb><rt>Ha-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族個</rb><rt>keh-dzoh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>一丘</rb><rt>ih-kyʽiu</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷個</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利羅熙</rb><rt>Yi-li-lo-hyi</rt><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Jing-ming, z Yi-seh-lih-go Jing-ming. 就是神明，是<u>以色列</u>個神明。（原註）</sup>

### Ts. XXXIV.

<u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生撥</rb><rt>sang-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底拿</rb><rt>Ti-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>哈抹</u>個</rb><rt>Ha-meh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示劍</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>引誘</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>Sing-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底拿</rb><rt>Ti-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>戀戀不捨</rb><rt>lin-lin-peh-shæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>安慰個</rb><rt>en-w̆e-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>示劍</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈抹</rb><rt>Ha-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底拿</rb><rt>Ti-nô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>閉口</rb><rt>pi-kʽeo</rt><rb>無言</rb><rt>vu-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb><u>示劍</u>個</rb><rt>Z-kyin-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈抹</rb><rt>Ha-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示劍</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>阿妹</rb><rt>ah-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底拿</rb><rt>Ti-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>調皮</rb><rt>diao-bi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈抹</rb><rt>Ha-meh</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>14</sup> <sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿妹個</rb><rt>ah-me-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>闖進</rb><rt>cʽông-tsing</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>衆人個</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搶奪</rb><rt>tsʽiang-deh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>Pô</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>帶勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西緬</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>連累</rb><rt>lin-le</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>賊臭個</rb><rt>zah-tsʽiu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>合家</rb><rt>ʽeh-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>阿妹</rb><rt>ah-me</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>妓女</rb><rt>gyi-nyü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

### Ts. XXXV.

<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看個</rb><rt>kʽen-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>所有個</rb><rt>sô-yiu-go</rt><rb>外國</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>潔潔淨淨</rb><rt>kyih-kyih-zing-zing</rt><rb>換勒</rb><rt>wun-leh</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>落難個</rb><rt>loh-næn-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>接應</rb><rt>tsih-ing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走路</rb><rt>tseo-lu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>保佑</rb><rt>pao-yiu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>所有個</rb><rt>sô-yiu-go</rt><rb>外國</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耳朵裏個</rb><rt>ng-to-li-go</rt><rb>環子</rb><rt>gwæn-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>囥來</rb><rt>kʽông-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>示劍</u>個</rb><rt>Z-kyin-go</rt><rb>橡樹</rb><rt>ziang-jü</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>威嚴</rb><rt>w̆e-nyin</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djoh</rt><rb>團圈個</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>追趕</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路斯</rb><rt>Lu-s</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>壇</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>益伯特利</rb><rt>Ih-pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb><u>利百加</u>個</rb><rt>Li-pah-kyüô-go</rt><rb>阿哺</rb><rt>ah-bu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底破拉</rb><rt>Ti-pʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葬來</rb><rt>tsông-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯特利</rb><rt>Pah-deh-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>橡樹</rb><rt>ziang-jü</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫巴哭</rb><rt>Üô-leng-pô-kʽoh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。

<u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葬來</rb><rt>tsông-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法大</rb><rt>Yi-fah-da</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恆</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拉結</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-kyih-go</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>墳個</rb><rt>veng-go</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以特</rb><rt>Yi-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>塔</rb><rt>tʽah</rt><rb>外邊</rb><rt>nga-pin</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>阿姨</rb><rt>ah-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>辟拉</rb><rt>Pih-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Ngô iu-meng-go ng-ts. 就是我憂悶個兒子。（原註）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西緬</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以薩迦</rb><rt>Yi-sah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西布倫</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>但</rb><rt>Dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>納大利</rb><rt>Neh-da-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拉結</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-kyih-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>辟拉</rb><rt>Pih-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞設</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>利亞</u>個</rb><rt>Li-üô-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>悉帕</rb><rt>Sih-pʽô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴坦亞蘭</rb><rt>Pô-tʽæn-üô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>幔利</rb><rt>Mæn-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞巴</rb><rt>Üô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯崙</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>耽擱個</rb><rt>tæn-koh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>八十</rb><rt>pah-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斷氣</rb><rt>dön-kyʽi</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>族種</rb><rt>dzoh-cong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. XXXVI.

<u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>家譜</rb><rt>kô-pu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞大</rb><rt>Üô-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以倫</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-leng-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞何利巴瑪</rb><rt>Üô-ʽo-li-pô-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>祭便</u>個</rb><rt>Tsi-bin-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴實抹</rb><rt>Pô-jih-meh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以實瑪利</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>尼徘約</u>個</rb><rt>Nyi-bæ-iah-go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞大</rb><rt>Üô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利法</rb><rt>Yi-li-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>巴實抹</rb><rt>Pô-jih-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流珥</rb><rt>Liu-r</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞何利巴瑪</rb><rt>Üô-ʽo-li-pô-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶是</rb><rt>Yiæ-z</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅蘭</rb><rt>Yüô-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>可拉</rb><rt>Kʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以掃</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sao-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>別𡍲</rb><rt>bih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>富足</rb><rt>fu-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉弗過</rb><rt>deng-feh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>耽擱個</rb><rt>tæn-koh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗能夠</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>容受</rb><rt>yüong-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西珥</rb><rt>Si-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>家譜</rb><rt>kô-pu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以利法</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-li-fah-go</rt><rb>阿姨</rb><rt>ah-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亭訥</rb><rt>Ding-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利法</rb><rt>Yi-li-fah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞大</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-da-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb><u>流珥</u>個</rb><rt>Liu-r-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿轄</rb><rt>Nô-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>沙瑪</rb><rt>Sô-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>米撒</rb><rt>Mi-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴實抹</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-jih-meh-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以掃</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sao-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞何利巴瑪</rb><rt>Üô-ʽo-li-pô-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>祭便</u>個</rb><rt>Tsi-bin-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶是</rb><rt>Yiæ-z</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅蘭</rb><rt>Yüô-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>可拉</rb><rt>Kʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>藩王個</rb><rt>væn-wông-go</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：<u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利法</rb><rt>Yi-li-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提幔</rb><rt>Di-mæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>阿抹</rb><rt>O-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>洗玻</rb><rt>Si-po</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>基納</rb><rt>Kyi-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>可拉</rb><rt>Kʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦但</rb><rt>Kyüô-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>亞瑪力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利法</rb><rt>Yi-li-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞大</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-da-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以掃</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sao-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流珥</rb><rt>Liu-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿轄</rb><rt>Nô-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>沙瑪</rb><rt>Sô-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>米撒</rb><rt>Mi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流珥</rb><rt>Liu-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴實抹</u>個</rb><rt>Pô-jih-meh-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞何利巴瑪</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-ʽo-li-pô-mô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶是</rb><rt>Yiæ-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>雅蘭</rb><rt>Yüô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>可拉</rb><rt>Kʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞拿</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-nô-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以掃</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sao-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞何利巴瑪</rb><rt>Üô-ʽo-li-pô-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>藩王個</rb><rt>væn-wông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以東</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-tong-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>藩王個</rb><rt>væn-wông-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>何利</rb><rt>ʽO-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西珥</u>個</rb><rt>Si-r-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅單</rb><rt>Lo-tæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>說八</rb><rt>Shih-pah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>祭便</rb><rt>Tsi-bin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>底順</rb><rt>Ti-jing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以察</rb><rt>Yi-tsʽah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>底珊</rb><rt>Ti-sæn</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何利</rb><rt>ʽO-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>西珥</u>個</rb><rt>Si-r-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb><u>羅單</u>個</rb><rt>Lo-tæn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何利</rb><rt>ʽO-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希幔</rb><rt>Hyi-mæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>羅單</u>個</rb><rt>Lo-tæn-go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亭訥</rb><rt>Ding-neh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>底順</rb><rt>Ti-jing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>以察</rb><rt>Yi-tsʽah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>底珊</rb><rt>Ti-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西珥</rb><rt>Si-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>何利</rb><rt>ʽO-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以東</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-tong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb><u>比珥</u>個</rb><rt>Pi-r-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比拉</rb><rt>Pi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以東</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-tong-go</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亭哈巴</rb><rt>Ding-ha-pô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>比拉</rb><rt>Pi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>破斯拉</rb><rt>Pʽo-s-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西拉</u>個</rb><rt>Si-lah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約八</rb><rt>Iah-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>接位</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup>
