<u><ruby><rb>戶珊</rb><rt>Wu-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>比撻</u>個</rb><rt>Pi-tʽah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈撻</rb><rt>Ha-tʽah</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩押</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-iah-go</rt><rb>平垟</rb><rt>bing-yiang</rt><rb>打敗</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>接位</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞未得</rb><rt>Üô-vi-teh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哈撻</rb><rt>Ha-tʽah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>瑪士利加</rb><rt>Mô-z-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>三拉</rb><rt>Sæn-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>接位</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>三拉</rb><rt>Sæn-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>邊沿個</rb><rt>pin-yin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利河伯</rb><rt>Li-ʽo-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>接位</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亞革破</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-keh-pʽo-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒哈南</rb><rt>Pô-leh-ha-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>接位</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb><u>亞革破</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-keh-pʽo-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴勒哈南</rb><rt>Pô-leh-ha-nen</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>哈達</rb><rt>Ha-dah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>接位</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保</rb><rt>Pao</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米希大別</rb><rt>Mi-hyi-da-bih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米色合</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-seh-ʽeh-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>瑪特列</rb><rt>Mô-deh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族</rb><rt>keh-dzoh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>住居</rb><rt>djü-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>：<u><ruby><rb>亭拿</rb><rt>Ding-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>亞勒華</rb><rt>Üô-leh-wô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>耶帖</rb><rt>Yiæ-tʽiah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞何利巴瑪</rb><rt>Üô-ʽo-li-pô-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>以拉</rb><rt>Yi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>比嫩</rb><rt>Pi-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基納</rb><rt>Kyi-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>提幔</rb><rt>Di-mæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>蜜薩</rb><rt>Mih-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>麥鐵</rb><rt>Mah-tʽih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>以蘭</rb><rt>Yi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以東</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-tong-go</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>基業個</rb><rt>kyi-nyih-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>庉間</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以東</rb><rt>Yi-tong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以掃</rb><rt>Yi-sao</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文此處錯印爲 Pô-lah-ha-nen，前文爲 Pô-leh-ha-nen.</sup>

---

### Ts. XXXVII.

<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>耽擱個</rb><rt>tæn-koh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>：<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十七</rb><rt>jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>看羊</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>阿姨</rb><rt>ah-yi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>辟拉</rb><rt>Pih-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>悉帕個</rb><rt>Sih-pʽô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>錯處</rb><rt>tsʽo-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>過如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一件</rb><rt>ih-gyin</rt><rb>繡花個</rb><rt>siu-hwô-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>過如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>和睦個</rb><rt>ʽo-moh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>束稻</rb><rt>shoh-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一束</rb><rt>ih-shoh</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt><rb>筆直</rb><rt>pih-dzih</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>束個</rb><rt>shoh-go</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>束</rb><rt>shoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>管治</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>十一粒</rb><rt>jih-ih-lih</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>責罰</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗過</rb><rt>feh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>存的</rb><rt>dzeng-tih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示劍</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>羊隊</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示劍</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>羊隊</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯崙</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嶴</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示劍</rb><rt>Z-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>田畈裏</rb><rt>din-pæn-li</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>羊個</rb><rt>yiang-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>多單</rb><rt>To-tæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>多單</rb><rt>To-tæn</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>Feh-zing</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>謀殺</rb><rt>meo-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>講來</rb><rt>kông-læ</rt><rb>講去</rb><rt>kông-kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>夢個</rb><rt>mông-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>地坑裏</rb><rt>di-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>惡惡個</rb><rt>ôh-ôh-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>拖喫了</rb><rt>tʽa-kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>應驗</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>傷</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地坑裏</rb><rt>di-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>剝</rb><rt>poh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>穿的個</rb><rt>cʽün-tih-go</rt><rb>繡花個</rb><rt>siu-hwô-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>Pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地坑裏</rb><rt>di-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地坑</rb><rt>di-kʽang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>空個</rb><rt>kʽong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-loh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>列隊</rb><rt>lih-de</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基列</rb><rt>Kyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>乳香</rb><rt>jü-hyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>沒藥</rb><rt>meh-yiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>囥過</rb><rt>kʽông-ko</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>骨肉</rb><rt>kweh-joh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>個</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>客商</rb><rt>kʽah-shông</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地坑裏</rb><rt>di-kʽang-li</rt><rb>拉出</rb><rt>l̆a-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱歸</rb><rt>do-kyü</rt><rb>廿塊</rb><rt>nyiæn-kwʽe</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>地坑</rb><rt>di-kʽang</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地坑裏</rb><rt>di-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扯開</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>走轉</rb><rt>Tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋弄</rb><rt>dza-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄山羊</rb><rt>yüong-sæn-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>染勒</rb><rt>nyin-leh</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>Pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>繡花</rb><rt>siu-hwô</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>帶撥</rb><rt>ta-peh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>認得個</rb><rt>nying-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>惡惡個</rb><rt>ôh-ôh-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>咬煞了</rb><rt>ngao-sah-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扯開</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>束</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>腰</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>傷心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>悲傷</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>陰司裏</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>號淚大哭</rb><rt>ʽao-li-da-kʽoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>波提乏</rb><rt>Po-di-veh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>侍衛個</rb><rt>z-we-go</rt><rb>頭腦</rb><rt>deo-nao</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. XXXVIII.

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞土蘭</rb><rt>Üô-tʽu-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希拉</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>書亞</u>個</rb><rt>Shü-üô-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>受胎</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>珥</rb><rt>R</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>受胎</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿南</rb><rt>O-nen</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示拉</rb><rt>Z-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基悉</rb><rt>Kyi-sih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>珥</rb><rt>R</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大瑪</rb><rt>Da-mô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>7</sup> <sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>過勒</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>書亞</u>個</rb><rt>Shü-üô-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞土蘭</rb><rt>Üô-tʽu-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希拉</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亭訥</rb><rt>Ding-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>剪</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>羊毛</rb><rt>yiang-mao</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大瑪</rb><rt>Da-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿公</rb><rt>ah-kong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亭訥</rb><rt>Ding-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>剪</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>羊毛</rb><rt>yiang-mao</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大瑪</rb><rt>Da-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示拉</rb><rt>Z-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>成人</rb><rt>dzing-nying</rt><rb>長大</rb><rt>tsiang-da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>嫁撥</rb><rt>kô-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>寡婦個</rb><rt>kwô-vu-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>手帕</rb><rt>siu-pʽao</rt><rb>蓋勒</rb><rt>kæ-leh</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐來</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亭訥</u>個</rb><rt>Ding-neh-go</rt><rb>路邊沿</rb><rt>lu-pin-yin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩埭</rb><rt>liang-da</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>蓋勒</rb><rt>kæ-leh</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>妓女</rb><rt>gyi-nyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路街</rb><rt>lu-ka</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>羊隊裏個</rb><rt>yiang-de-li-go</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>當頭</rb><rt>tông-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>當頭</rb><rt>tông-deo</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>手裏個</rb><rt>siu-li-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>受胎了</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大瑪</rb><rt>Da-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>蓋</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>面孔個</rb><rt>min-kʽong-go</rt><rb>手帕</rb><rt>siu-pʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>寡婦個</rb><rt>kwô-vu-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>走轉</rb><rt>Tseo-cün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗着</rb><rt>feh-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>妓女</rb><rt>gyi-nyü</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>23</sup> <sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>過勒</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大瑪</rb><rt>Da-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>妓女</rb><rt>gyi-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受胎了</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用拉</rb><rt>Yüong-l̆a</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>拉出個</rb><rt>l̆a-cʽih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿公</rb><rt>ah-kong</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇東西</rb><rt>Keh-tong-si</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>居主</rb><rt>kæ-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>受胎</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>示拉</rb><rt>Z-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生姆</rb><rt>sang-m</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>胎裏</rb><rt>tʽæ-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>雙生</rb><rt>shông-sang</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>臨</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>收生</rb><rt>siu-sang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>紅線</rb><rt>ʽong-sin</rt><rb>縛</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>跔進</rb><rt>geo-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>生出來了</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>收生個</rb><rt>Siu-sang-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>𧼰出來個</rb><rt>zah-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>𧼰出來</rb><rt>zah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法勒士</rb><rt>Fah-leh-z</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>紅線</rb><rt>ʽong-sin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* dza zah-cʽih-læ. 就是咋𧼰出來。（原註）</sup>

### Ts. XXXIX.

<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>帶到</rb><rt>ta-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>波提乏</rb><rt>Po-di-veh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>侍衛</rb><rt>z-we</rt><rb>頭腦</rb><rt>deo-nao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以實瑪利</rb><rt>Yi-jih-mô-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>順流</rb><rt>jing-liu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>順流</rb><rt>jing-liu</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>總管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>總管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>朵化</rb><rt>tô-hwô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>所有</rb><rt>sô-yiu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曉得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>俏麗</rb><rt>tsʽiao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家個</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>主人家個</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>Oh-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗曉得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所有個</rb><rt>sô-yiu-go</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>Oh-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>家人</rb><rt>kô-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>拉</rb><rt>l̆a</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>出去了</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>家人</rb><rt>kô-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>帶來個</rb><rt>ta-læ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走進來</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt><rb>調戲</rb><rt>diao-hyi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>同牀</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>懷懷</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>謳響</rb><rt>eo-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>逃出去了</rb><rt>dao-cʽih-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>帶來個</rb><rt>ta-læ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>調戲</rb><rt>diao-hyi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>懷懷</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>謳響</rb><rt>eo-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逃出去了</rb><rt>dao-cʽih-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>火氣</rb><rt>ho-kyʽi</rt><rb>直衝</rb><rt>dzih-cʽong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落監</rb><rt>loh-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢監裏了</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>司獄司個</rb><rt>s-nyüoh-s-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>司獄司</rb><rt>S-nyüoh-s</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>牢監裏個</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>司獄司</rb><rt>S-nyüoh-s</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手下個</rb><rt>siu-ʽô-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所做個</rb><rt>sô-tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>順流</rb><rt>jing-liu</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ts. XL.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王個</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>酒政</rb><rt>tsiu-tsing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>膳長</rb><rt>jün-tsiang</rt><rb>觸犯</rb><rt>cʽoh-væn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>酒政</rb><rt>tsiu-tsing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>膳長</rb><rt>jün-tsiang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>挈</rb><rt>Kyʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落監</rb><rt>loh-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>關間個</rb><rt>kwæn-kæn-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>侍衛</rb><rt>z-we</rt><rb>頭腦</rb><rt>deo-nao</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>侍衛</rb><rt>Z-we</rt><rb>頭腦</rb><rt>deo-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>託付</rb><rt>tʽoh-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>幾日</rb><rt>kyi-nyih</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王個</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>酒政</rb><rt>tsiu-tsing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>膳長</rb><rt>jün-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>關來</rb><rt>kwæn-læ</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做夜</rb><rt>tso-yia</rt><rb>得</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>講究個</rb><rt>kông-kyiu-go</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>關來</rb><rt>kwæn-læ</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>解說</rb><rt>Ka-shih</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>酒政</rb><rt>Tsiu-tsing</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>夢裏</rb><rt>mông-li</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三乓</rb><rt>sæn-pʽang</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>抽芽</rb><rt>tsʽiu-ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>開花</rb><rt>kʽæ-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>葡萄籽</rb><rt>bu-dao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>球打球</rb><rt>gyiu-tang-gyiu</rt><rb>熟了</rb><rt>joh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>酒杯</rb><rt>tsiu-pe</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>摘</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>榨</rb><rt>tsô</rt><rb>汁水</rb><rt>tsih-shü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>酒杯裏</rb><rt>tsiu-pe-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>捧勒</rb><rt>pʽong-leh</rt><rb>酒杯</rb><rt>tsiu-pe</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夢個</rb><rt>Mông-go</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三乓</rb><rt>sæn-pʽang</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>三日</rb><rt>Sæn-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起來</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>復</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>捧</rb><rt>pʽong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>酒杯</rb><rt>tsiu-pe</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>酒政</rb><rt>tsiu-tsing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>奏明</rb><rt>tseo-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>落監個</rb><rt>loh-kæn-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>膳長</rb><rt>Jün-tsiang</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>夢裏</rb><rt>mông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三隻</rb><rt>sæn-tsah</rt><rb>白籃</rb><rt>bah-læn</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>頭頂</rb><rt>deo-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>箇隻</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>籃</rb><rt>læn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>廚工</rb><rt>djü-kong</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>煮撥</rb><rt>ts-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>下飯</rb><rt>ʽô-væn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>頭頂</rb><rt>deo-teng</rt><rb>籃裏</rb><rt>læn-li</rt><rb>啄</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>三隻</rb><rt>sæn-tsah</rt><rb>籃</rb><rt>læn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>三日</rb><rt>Sæn-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吊</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>樹裏</rb><rt>jü-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>啄</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生日</rb><rt>sang-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>百官</rb><rt>pah-kwun</rt><rb>辦酒</rb><rt>bæn-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百官</rb><rt>pah-kwun</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>酒政</rb><rt>tsiu-tsing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>膳長</rb><rt>jün-tsiang</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起來</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>Wæn</rt><rb>酒政個</rb><rt>tsiu-tsing-go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>捧勒</rb><rt>pʽong-leh</rt><rb>酒杯</rb><rt>tsiu-pe</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吊煞</rb><rt>tiao-sah</rt><rb>膳長</rb><rt>jün-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>酒政</rb><rt>tsiu-tsing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>忘記</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

### Ts. XLI.

<ruby><rb>兩</rb><rt>Liang</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做夜</rb><rt>tso-yia</rt><rb>得</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>講究</rb><rt>kông-kyiu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑟納巴尼</rb><rt>Seh-neh-pô-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>安</u>個</rb><rt>En-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>波提非拉</u>個</rb><rt>Po-di-fi-lah-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西納</rb><rt>Üô-si-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>巡邏</rb><rt>jing-lo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>巡邏</rb><rt>jing-lo</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>大熟個</rb><rt>da-joh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地土個</rb><rt>di-tʽu-go</rt><rb>出產</rb><rt>cʽih-tsʽæn</rt><rb>茂茂盛盛</rb><rt>meo-meo-zing-zing</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>收攏</rb><rt>siu-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>大熟個</rb><rt>da-joh-go</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>積聚</rb><rt>tsih-jü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>dzing-nga</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>糧草</rb><rt>liang-tsʽao</rt><rb>積聚</rb><rt>tsih-jü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>收攏個</rb><rt>siu-long-go</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>沙泥</rb><rt>sô-nyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>計</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>量弗來個</rb><rt>liang-feh-læ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>荒年</rb><rt>Hwông-nyin</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>安</u>個</rb><rt>En-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>波提非拉</u>個</rb><rt>Po-di-fi-lah-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西納</rb><rt>Üô-si-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生撥</rb><rt>sang-peh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子個</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忘記</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>Cʽü</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法蓮</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>受苦個</rb><rt>ziu-kwʽu-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>收成</rb><rt>siu-dzing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Peh-gyi mông-kyi. 就是撥其忘記。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z* Yiu siu-ddzing. 就是有收成。（原註）</sup>

<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>七年</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyin</rt><rb>大熟</rb><rt>da-joh</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>七個</rb><rt>Tsʽih-go</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>接上</rb><rt>tsih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>55</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>遍</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>穀倉</rb><rt>koh-tsʽông</rt><rb>糶撥</rb><rt>tʽiao-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>各國個</rb><rt>koh-koh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>糴</rb><rt>dih</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ts. XLII.

<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>看東</rb><rt>kʽen-tong</rt></ruby>？」<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>糴</rb><rt>dih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>糴</rb><rt>dih</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>Kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>糴穀個</rb><rt>dih-koh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>穀價</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>糶</rb><rt>tʽiao</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裝勒</rb><rt>tsông-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>糧食個</rb><rt>liang-zih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>探子</rb><rt>tʽen-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>破綻</rb><rt>pʽo-dzæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>探子</rb><rt>tʽen-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>破綻個</rb><rt>pʽo-dzæn-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>頂小個</rb><rt>ting-siao-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話過了</rb><rt>wô-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>探子</rb><rt>tʽen-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>陪</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>關來</rb><rt>kwæn-læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>探子</rb><rt>tʽen-ts</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>關了</rb><rt>kwæn-leh</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>關來</rb><rt>kwæn-læ</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>載勒</rb><rt>tsæ-leh</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>急</rb><rt>kyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <sup>21</sup> <sup>25</sup> <sup>29</sup>
