<ruby><rb>本來</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>嘸得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>今末子</rb><rt>kyih-mih-ts</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小個</rb><rt>siao-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。』<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>載勒</rb><rt>tsæ-leh</rt><rb>糧草</rb><rt>liang-tsʽao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>急</rb><rt>kyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>陪</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>探子</rb><rt>tʽen-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>袋</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>倒出個</rb><rt>tao-cʽih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人個</rb><rt>koh-nying-go</rt><rb>銀包</rb><rt>nying-pao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>袋裏</rb><rt>dæ-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>銀包</rb><rt>nying-pao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>喫𢞕了</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>害煞</rb><rt>ʽæ-sah</rt><rb>我了</rb><rt>ngô-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西緬</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>帶來</rb><rt>ta-læ</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>帶來</rb><rt>ta-læ</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>獨自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>孤孤悽悽</rb><rt>kwu-kwu-tsʽi-tsʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>陰司裏</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

---


### Ts. XLIII.

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱來個</rb><rt>do-læ-go</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>完了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>買點</rb><rt>ma-tin</rt><rb>糧草</rb><rt>liang-tsʽao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>結結實實</rb><rt>kyih-kyih-jih-jih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>。』<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>糧草</rb><rt>liang-tsʽao</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>細細</rb><rt>si-si</rt><rb>底底</rb><rt>ti-ti</rt><rb>盤問</rb><rt>bun-meng</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>履歷</rb><rt>li-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>問個</rb><rt>meng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>Ta</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>Tsʽa</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>保</rb><rt>pao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>討還</rb><rt>tʽao-wæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt><rb>安來</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>捱捱棲棲</rb><rt>nga-nga-tsʽi-tsʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去仔</rb><rt>kyʽi-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>乳香</rb><rt>jü-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖</rb><rt>mih-dông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>沒藥</rb><rt>meh-yiah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>榧子</rb><rt>fi-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>杏仁</rb><rt>ʽang-nying</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶落去</rb><rt>ta-loh-kyʽi</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>Siu-li</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>一倍</rb><rt>ih-be</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>袋裏</rb><rt>dæ-li</rt><rb>帶歸來個</rb><rt>ta-kyü-læ-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>帶去</rb><rt>ta-kyʽi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>舛錯</rb><rt>cʽün-tsʽoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>陪</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>全能個</rb><rt>djün-neng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>喪掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>只好</rb><rt>tsih-hao</rt><rb>喪掉了</rb><rt>sông-diao-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>加倍個</rb><rt>kô-be-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>總管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>晝過</rb><rt>tsiu-ko</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫飯</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>頭遭</rb><rt>deo-tsao</rt><rb>袋裏</rb><rt>dæ-li</rt><rb>帶歸個</rb><rt>ta-kyü-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>扳頭</rb><rt>pæn-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奪</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>糧草</rb><rt>liang-tsʽao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>袋裏</rb><rt>dæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>袋裏</rb><rt>dæ-li</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>收到了</rb><rt>siu-tao-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西緬</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>見面</rb><rt>kyin-min</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>餵</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晝過</rb><rt>tsiu-ko</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>喫飯</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>健個</rb><rt>gyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>傴帶倒</rb><rt>eo-tæn-tao</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>同母</rb><rt>dong-meo</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>哭個</rb><rt>kʽoh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>房裏</rb><rt>vông-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>滰了</rb><rt>Gyiang-leh</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做其</rb><rt>tso-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>擺</rb><rt>Pa</rt><rb>下飯</rb><rt>ʽô-væn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>擺起來</rb><rt>pa-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>一桌</rb><rt>ih-coh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>一桌</rb><rt>ih-coh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一桌</rb><rt>ih-coh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>同席</rb><rt>dong-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同席</rb><rt>dong-zih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>惡愫個</rb><rt>u-su-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>派</rb><rt>Pʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>坐位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>大小個</rb><rt>do-siao-go</rt><rb>次序</rb><rt>tsʽ-jü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>菜蔬</rb><rt>tsʽæ-su</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>便雅憫</u>個</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming-go</rt><rb>菜蔬</rb><rt>tsʽæ-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>四倍</rb><rt>s-be</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫酒</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tsiu</rt><rb>快樂</rb><rt>kwʽa-loh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文此處錯印爲 Iah-sah，前後文均爲 Iah-seh</sup>

---

### Ts. XLIV.

<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>總管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>𣥼滿</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>袋裏</rb><rt>dæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>揹勒動個</rb><rt>pe-leh-dong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>各人個</rb><rt>koh-nying-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>袋口裏</rb><rt>dæ-kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>爵杯</rb><rt>tsiah-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>爵杯</rb><rt>tsiah-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>糴穀個</rb><rt>dih-koh-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小個</rb><rt>siao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>袋口裏</rb><rt>dæ-kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所話</rb><rt>sô-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>剛剛</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>出城</rb><rt>Cʽih-dzing</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗遠</rb><rt>feh-yün</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>總管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>追</rb><rt>tse</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>追着仔</rb><rt>tse-djoh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>善</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>報</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>豈弗是</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>爵杯</rb><rt>tsiah-pe</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>起課個</rb><rt>kyʽi-kʽo-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>作惡</rb><rt>tsoh-ôh</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>追着</rb><rt>tse-djoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>斷</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>袋口裏</rb><rt>dæ-kʽeo-li</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>帶來</rb><rt>ta-læ</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>cü-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>嘸罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>袋</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>放落</rb><rt>fông-loh</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>解開</rb><rt>ka-kʽæ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>袋</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>總管</rb><rt>Tsong-kwun</rt><rb>搜</rb><rt>seo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>小個</rb><rt>siao-go</rt><rb>爲止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djoh</rt><rb>爵杯</rb><rt>tsiah-pe</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>袋裏</rb><rt>dæ-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扯開</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>揹個</rb><rt>pe-go</rt><rb>放來</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>撲倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所做</rb><rt>sô-tso</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>起課個</rb><rt>kyʽi-kʽo-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>辯</rb><rt>bin</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>奴僕個</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djoh</rt><rb>爵杯個</rb><rt>tsiah-pe-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>cü-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>斷</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djoh</rt><rb>爵杯個</rb><rt>tsiah-pe-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安安穩穩</rb><rt>en-en-w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>生火</rb><rt>sang-ho</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？』<sup>20</sup> ### Ts. XLV.

<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>五年</rb><rt>ng-nyin</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>）。<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>家眷</rb><rt>kô-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>缺少長短</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao-dziang-tön</rt></ruby>。』<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>活間</rb><rt>weh-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心灰</rb><rt>sing-hwe</rt><rb>意懶</rb><rt>i-læn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差來</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>活動了</rb><rt>weh-dong-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夠了</rb><rt>Keo-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Ts. XLVI.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所有個</rb><rt>sô-yiu-go</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別是巴</rb><rt>Bih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>獻祭</rb><rt>hyin-tsi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>Yia-tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>！」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>妨得</rb><rt>fông-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-loh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>親手</rb><rt>tsʽing-siu</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別是巴</rb><rt>Bih-z-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差來</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>載</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>家小</rb><rt>kô-siao</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djoh-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>貨物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>孫子</rb><rt>seng-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>孫女</rb><rt>seng-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb><u>流便</u>個</rb><rt>Liu-bin-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>哈諾</rb><rt>Ha-noh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>法路</rb><rt>Fah-lu</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希斯崙</rb><rt>Hyi-s-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>迦米</rb><rt>Kyüô-mi</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb><u>西緬</u>個</rb><rt>Si-min-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶母利</rb><rt>Yiæ-meo-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅憫</rb><rt>Yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>阿轄</rb><rt>O-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅斤</rb><rt>Yüô-kying</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>瑣轄</rb><rt>So-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>少羅</rb><rt>Siao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb><u>利未</u>個</rb><rt>Li-vi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>革順</rb><rt>Keh-jing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哥轄</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>米拉利</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb><u>猶大</u>個</rb><rt>Yiu-da-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>珥</rb><rt>R</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>阿南</rb><rt>O-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>示拉</rb><rt>Z-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>法勒士</rb><rt>Fah-leh-z</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>撒拉</rb><rt>Sah-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>珥</rb><rt>R</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿南</rb><rt>O-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死來</rb><rt>si-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方了</rb><rt>di-fông-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>法勒士</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-leh-z-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希斯崙</rb><rt>Hyi-s-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈母勒</rb><rt>Ha-meo-leh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以薩迦</u><sup> </sup>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-kyüô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>陀拉</rb><rt>Do-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>孚亞</rb><rt>Fu-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>約伯</rb><rt>Iah-pah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>伸崙</rb><rt>Sing-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb><u>西布倫</u>個</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西烈</rb><rt>Si-lih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以倫</rb><rt>Yi-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅利</rb><rt>Yüô-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利亞</rb><rt>Li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴坦亞蘭</rb><rt>Pô-tʽæn-üô-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>生撥</rb><rt>sang-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底拿</rb><rt>Ti-nô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>統共</rb><rt>tʽong-gong</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>三十三</rb><rt>sæn-jih-sæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[2\] 原文此處錯印爲 Yi-seh-kyüô，前後文均爲 Yi-sah-kyüô.</sup>

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb><u>迦得</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>洗非翁</rb><rt>Si-fi-ong</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哈基</rb><rt>Ha-kyi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>如尼</rb><rt>Jü-nyi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以士本</rb><rt>Yi-z-peng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利</rb><rt>Yi-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞羅底</rb><rt>Üô-lo-ti</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞利利</rb><rt>Üô-li-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb><u>亞設</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-shih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>音拿</rb><rt>Ing-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以書亞</rb><rt>Yi-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以書以</rb><rt>Yi-shü-yi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比利亞</rb><rt>Pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿妹</rb><rt>ah-me</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>比利亞</u>個</rb><rt>Pi-li-üô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希百</rb><rt>Hyi-pah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>麥結</rb><rt>Mah-kyih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>悉帕個</rb><rt>Sih-pʽô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>利亞</u>個</rb><rt>Li-üô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生撥</rb><rt>sang-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>統共</rb><rt>tʽong-gong</rt><rb>十六個</rb><rt>jih-loh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拉結</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-kyih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>安</u>個</rb><rt>En-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>波提非拉</u>個</rb><rt>Po-di-fi-lah-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西納</rb><rt>Üô-si-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生撥</rb><rt>sang-peh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以法蓮</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</u><sup> </sup>個</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>比拉</rb><rt>Pi-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比結</rb><rt>Pi-kyih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞實別</rb><rt>Üô-jih-bih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>其剌</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>乃幔</rb><rt>Næ-mæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以希</rb><rt>Yi-hyi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>羅實</rb><rt>Lo-jih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>母平</rb><rt>Meo-bing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>戶平</rb><rt>Wu-bing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞勒</rb><rt>Üô-leh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生撥</rb><rt>sang-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>統共</rb><rt>tʽong-gong</rt><rb>十四個</rb><rt>jih-s-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文此處錯印爲 Bin-üô-ming，前後文均爲 Bin-yüô-ming.</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb><u>但</u>個</rb><rt>Dæn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>戶伸</rb><rt>Wu-sing</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb><u>納大利</u>個</rb><rt>Neh-da-li-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅薛</rb><rt>Yüô-sih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>沽尼</rb><rt>Kwu-nyi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶色</rb><rt>Yiæ-seh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>示臉</rb><rt>Z-lin</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>辟拉</u>個</rb><rt>Pih-lah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉班</rb><rt>Lah-pæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拉結</u>個</rb><rt>Lah-kyih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生撥</rb><rt>sang-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>統共</rb><rt>tʽong-gong</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腰縛裏</rb><rt>iao-bo-li</rt><rb>生落來個</rb><rt>sang-loh-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>統共</rb><rt>tʽong-gong</rt><rb>六十六個</rb><rt>loh-jih-loh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>統共</rb><rt>tʽong-gong</rt><rb>七十個</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>備勒</rb><rt>be-leh</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>頭頸</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭頸沿</rb><rt>deo-kying-yin</rt><rb>哭了</rb><rt>kʽoh-leh</rt><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>安心</rb><rt>en-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>見面了</rb><rt>kyin-min-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>奏明</rb><rt>tseo-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>餵</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>爲業</rb><rt>we-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>牛羊</rb><rt>ngeo-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>Tʽông-jün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事業</rb><rt>z-nyih</rt></ruby>？』<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小來</rb><rt>siao-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>爲業</rb><rt>we-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>惡愫個</rb><rt>u-su-go</rt></ruby>。』」

---

### Ts. XLVII.

<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>奏明</rb><rt>tseo-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事業</rb><rt>z-nyih</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>草料</rb><rt>tsʽao-liao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>餵</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>揀</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>厚實個</rb><rt>ʽeo-jih-go</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>住來</rb><rt>djü-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>才能個</rb><rt>dzæ-neng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>舉薦</rb><rt>kyü-tsin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>貴庚</rb><rt>kwe-kang</rt></ruby>？」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>及如</rb><rt>gyih-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>揀</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>厚實</rb><rt>ʽeo-jih</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>蘭塞</rb><rt>Læn-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>老小</rb><rt>lao-siao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>家打家</rb><rt>kô-tang-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt><rb>困苦</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kwʽu</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>糶</rb><rt>tʽiao</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收攏</rb><rt>siu-long</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>馱進</rb><rt>do-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王宮裏</rb><rt>wông-kong-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>換了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>換了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>換了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Koh-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>牽</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>：<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>調</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇年</rb><rt>keh-nyin</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇年</rb><rt>Keh-nyin</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>弗敢</rb><rt>feh-ken</rt><rb>隱瞞</rb><rt>ing-mun</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>換了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主了</rb><rt>cü-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>cü-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>毀掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法</rb><rt>Fah</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>荒過</rb><rt>hwông-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>遍</rb><rt>pin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt><rb>追落來</rb><rt>tse-loh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>了</rb><rt>Fah-lao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>址界</rb><rt>ts-ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>址界個</rb><rt>ts-ka-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>搬進</rb><rt>pun-tsing</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>祭司個</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一定個</rb><rt>ih-ding-go</rt><rb>額子</rb><rt>ngah-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>俸祿</rb><rt>fong-loh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>種田</rb><rt>cong-din</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>出產</rb><rt>cʽih-tsʽæn</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>五股</rb><rt>ng-kwu</rt><rb>得一</rb><rt>teh-ih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>四股</rb><rt>s-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>箇星個</rb><rt>keh-sing-go</rt><rb>口糧</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>cü-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>遍</rb><rt>pin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>五股</rb><rt>ng-kwu</rt><rb>得一</rb><rt>teh-ih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>祭司個</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>生落來</rb><rt>sang-loh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>十七</rb><rt>jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>四十七</rb><rt>s-jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>快到</rb><rt>kwʽa-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>安來</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大腿下</rb><rt>do-tʽe-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>好意</rb><rt>hao-i</rt><rb>誠心</rb><rt>dzing-sing</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>做𡍲</rb><rt>tso-tʽah</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>葬間個</rb><rt>tsông-kæn-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>靠勒</rb><rt>kʽao-leh</rt><rb>眠牀</rb><rt>min-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>屏風</rb><rt>bing-fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>傴帶倒</rb><rt>eo-tæn-tao</rt></ruby>。
