### Ts. XLVIII.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以法蓮</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撐帶起</rb><rt>tsʽang-tæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐來</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt><rb>眠牀裏</rb><rt>min-zông-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路斯</rb><rt>Lu-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全能個</rb><rt>djün-neng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>茂茂</rb><rt>meo-meo</rt><rb>盛盛</rb><rt>zing-zing</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>將來個</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。』<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法蓮</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西緬</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴坦</rb><rt>Pô-tʽæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法大</rb><rt>Yi-fah-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拉結</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死來</rb><rt>si-læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法大</rb><rt>Yi-fah-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路裏</rb><rt>lu-li</rt></ruby>（<u><ruby><rb>以法大</rb><rt>Yi-fah-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恆</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u>）。」

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>攏</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>老花</rb><rt>lao-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>攏來</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>親</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗想</rb><rt>feh-siang</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>見面</rb><rt>kyin-min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>腳䯊頭</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安來</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以法蓮</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin-go</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>借隻</rb><rt>tsia-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>安來</rb><rt>en-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>瑪拿西</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>調錯</rb><rt>diao-tsʽo</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>走路個</rb><rt>tseo-lu-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>難頭個</rb><rt>næn-deo-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以法蓮</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>擋勒</rb><rt>tông-leh</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以法蓮</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin-go</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt><rb>趲過</rb><rt>tsæn-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>瑪拿西</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>弓</rb><rt>kong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>奪來個</rb><rt>deh-læ-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>一倍</rb><rt>ih-be</rt></ruby>。」

### Ts. XLIX.

<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>势道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>氣力個</rb><rt>kyʽi-lih-go</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>威風</rb><rt>w̆e-fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>弗定</rb><rt>feh-ding</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗能夠</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>好聚</rb><rt>hao-jü</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>眠牀</rb><rt>min-zông</rt><rb>污壞</rb><rt>u-wæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>眠牀了</rb><rt>min-zông-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西緬</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>寶劍</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>兇器</rb><rt>hyüong-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>聚</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>發怏</rb><rt>fah-ông</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>割斷</rb><rt>keh-dön</rt><rb>牛個</rb><rt>ngeo-go</rt><rb>腳筋</rb><rt>kyiah-kying</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>動怒</rb><rt>dong-nu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咒罵個</rb><rt>tsiu-mô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>猛</rb><rt>mang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>恨心</rb><rt>ʽeng-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咒罵個</rb><rt>tsiu-mô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>兇</rb><rt>hyüong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>分其出</rb><rt>feng-gyi-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>散其開</rb><rt>sæn-gyi-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>抲住</rb><rt>kʽô-djü</rt><rb>仇敵個</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>頭頸</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>穴</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>買來個</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>完了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>縮攏</rb><rt>shoh-long</rt><rb>眠牀裏</rb><rt>min-zông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>斷了</rb><rt>dön-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>族種</rb><rt>dzoh-cong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

---

### Ts. L.

<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>面孔裏</rb><rt>min-kʽong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>親</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屬下個</rb><rt>joh-ʽô-go</rt><rb>醫生</rb><rt>i-sang</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>鹽</rb><rt>yin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>醫生</rb><rt>i-sang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>鹽</rb><rt>yin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>鹽</rb><rt>yin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>日數</rb><rt>nyih-su</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>哭個</rb><rt>kʽoh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>奏明</rb><rt>tseo-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>掘出個</rb><rt>gyüih-cʽih-go</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>罰咒個</rb><rt>vah-tsiu-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牛羊</rb><rt>ngeo-yiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>騎馬個</rb><rt>gyi-mô-go</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>跟從</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亞達</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-dah-go</rt><rb>曬場</rb><rt>sa-dziang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>悲傷</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>號淚大哭</rb><rt>ʽao-li-da-kʽoh</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>悲傷</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞達</rb><rt>Üô-dah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曬場裏個</rb><rt>sa-dziang-li-go</rt><rb>悲傷</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>號淚大哭</rb><rt>ʽao-li-da-kʽoh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯麥西</rb><rt>Üô-pah-mah-si</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>抬</rb><rt>Dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葬來</rb><rt>tsông-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>幔利</u>個</rb><rt>Mæn-li-go</rt><rb>對面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>麥比拉</rb><rt>Mah-pi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>田裏個</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>穴</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>穴</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗崙</rb><rt>Yi-feh-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>買來</rb><rt>ma-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>墳地個</rb><rt>veng-di-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*Ziu-z* Yiæ-gyih nying-go di-kʽoh. 就是<u>埃及</u>人個啼哭。（原註）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>還報</rb><rt>wæn-pao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>囑託</rb><rt>coh-tʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt></ruby>。」』<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寬恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>錯處</rb><rt>tsʽo-cʽü</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>哭了</rb><rt>kʽoh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>保全許多</rb><rt>pao-djün-hyü-to</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>今末仔</rb><rt>kyih-mih-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壽歲</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>一百零十</rb><rt>ih-pah-ling-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以法蓮</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-fah-lin-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>瑪拿西</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪吉</rb><rt>Mô-kyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>抱來</rb><rt>bao-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>腳䯊頭</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照顧</rb><rt>tsiao-kwu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>照顧</rb><rt>tsiao-kwu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>骨頭</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>十</rb><rt>jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>鹽</rb><rt>yin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>落殮</rb><rt>loh-lin</rt></ruby>。
